 46 | /**
 47 |  * High-level MCP server that provides a simpler API for working with resources, tools, and prompts.
 48 |  * For advanced usage (like sending notifications or setting custom request handlers), use the underlying
 49 |  * Server instance available via the `server` property.
 50 |  */
 51 | export class McpServer {
 52 |   /**
 53 |    * The underlying Server instance, useful for advanced operations like sending notifications.
 54 |    */
 55 |   public readonly server: Server;
 56 | 
 57 |   private _registeredResources: { [uri: string]: RegisteredResource } = {};
 58 |   private _registeredResourceTemplates: {
 59 |     [name: string]: RegisteredResourceTemplate;
 60 |   } = {};
 61 |   private _registeredTools: { [name: string]: RegisteredTool } = {};
 62 |   private _registeredPrompts: { [name: string]: RegisteredPrompt } = {};
 63 | 
 64 |   constructor(serverInfo: Implementation, options?: ServerOptions) {
 65 |     this.server = new Server(serverInfo, options);
 66 |   }
 67 | 
 68 |   /**
 69 |    * Attaches to the given transport, starts it, and starts listening for messages.
 70 |    *
 71 |    * The `server` object assumes ownership of the Transport, replacing any callbacks that have already been set, and expects that it is the only user of the Transport instance going forward.
 72 |    */
 73 |   async connect(transport: Transport): Promise<void> {
 74 |     return await this.server.connect(transport);
 75 |   }
 76 | 
 77 |   /**
 78 |    * Closes the connection.
 79 |    */
 80 |   async close(): Promise<void> {
 81 |     await this.server.close();
 82 |   }
 83 | 
 84 |   private _toolHandlersInitialized = false;
 85 | 
 86 |   private setToolRequestHandlers() {
 87 |     if (this._toolHandlersInitialized) {
 88 |       return;
 89 |     }
 90 |     
 91 |     this.server.assertCanSetRequestHandler(
 92 |       ListToolsRequestSchema.shape.method.value,
 93 |     );
 94 |     this.server.assertCanSetRequestHandler(
 95 |       CallToolRequestSchema.shape.method.value,
 96 |     );
 97 | 
 98 |     this.server.registerCapabilities({
 99 |       tools: {},
100 |     });
101 | 
102 |     this.server.setRequestHandler(
103 |       ListToolsRequestSchema,
104 |       (): ListToolsResult => ({
105 |         tools: Object.entries(this._registeredTools).map(
106 |           ([name, tool]): Tool => {
107 |             return {
108 |               name,
109 |               description: tool.description,
110 |               inputSchema: tool.inputSchema
111 |                 ? (zodToJsonSchema(tool.inputSchema, {
112 |                     strictUnions: true,
113 |                   }) as Tool["inputSchema"])
114 |                 : EMPTY_OBJECT_JSON_SCHEMA,
115 |             };
116 |           },
117 |         ),
118 |       }),
119 |     );
120 | 
121 |     this.server.setRequestHandler(
122 |       CallToolRequestSchema,
123 |       async (request, extra): Promise<CallToolResult> => {
124 |         const tool = this._registeredTools[request.params.name];
125 |         if (!tool) {
126 |           throw new McpError(
127 |             ErrorCode.InvalidParams,
128 |             `Tool ${request.params.name} not found`,
129 |           );
130 |         }
131 | 
132 |         if (tool.inputSchema) {
133 |           const parseResult = await tool.inputSchema.safeParseAsync(
134 |             request.params.arguments,
135 |           );
136 |           if (!parseResult.success) {
137 |             throw new McpError(
138 |               ErrorCode.InvalidParams,
139 |               `Invalid arguments for tool ${request.params.name}: ${parseResult.error.message}`,
140 |             );
141 |           }
142 | 
143 |           const args = parseResult.data;
144 |           const cb = tool.callback as ToolCallback<ZodRawShape>;
145 |           try {
146 |             return await Promise.resolve(cb(args, extra));
147 |           } catch (error) {
148 |             return {
149 |               content: [
150 |                 {
151 |                   type: "text",
152 |                   text: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
153 |                 },
154 |               ],
155 |               isError: true,
156 |             };
157 |           }
158 |         } else {
159 |           const cb = tool.callback as ToolCallback<undefined>;
160 |           try {
161 |             return await Promise.resolve(cb(extra));
162 |           } catch (error) {
163 |             return {
164 |               content: [
165 |                 {
166 |                   type: "text",
167 |                   text: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
168 |                 },
169 |               ],
170 |               isError: true,
171 |             };
172 |           }
173 |         }
174 |       },
175 |     );
176 | 
177 |     this._toolHandlersInitialized = true;
178 |   }
179 | 
180 |   private _completionHandlerInitialized = false;
181 | 
182 |   private setCompletionRequestHandler() {
183 |     if (this._completionHandlerInitialized) {
184 |       return;
185 |     }
186 | 
187 |     this.server.assertCanSetRequestHandler(
188 |       CompleteRequestSchema.shape.method.value,
189 |     );
190 | 
191 |     this.server.setRequestHandler(
192 |       CompleteRequestSchema,
193 |       async (request): Promise<CompleteResult> => {
194 |         switch (request.params.ref.type) {
195 |           case "ref/prompt":
196 |             return this.handlePromptCompletion(request, request.params.ref);
197 | 
198 |           case "ref/resource":
199 |             return this.handleResourceCompletion(request, request.params.ref);
200 | 
201 |           default:
202 |             throw new McpError(
203 |               ErrorCode.InvalidParams,
204 |               `Invalid completion reference: ${request.params.ref}`,
205 |             );
206 |         }
207 |       },
208 |     );
209 | 
210 |     this._completionHandlerInitialized = true;
211 |   }
212 | 
213 |   private async handlePromptCompletion(
214 |     request: CompleteRequest,
215 |     ref: PromptReference,
216 |   ): Promise<CompleteResult> {
217 |     const prompt = this._registeredPrompts[ref.name];
218 |     if (!prompt) {
219 |       throw new McpError(
220 |         ErrorCode.InvalidParams,
221 |         `Prompt ${request.params.ref.name} not found`,
222 |       );
223 |     }
224 | 
225 |     if (!prompt.argsSchema) {
226 |       return EMPTY_COMPLETION_RESULT;
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     const field = prompt.argsSchema.shape[request.params.argument.name];
230 |     if (!(field instanceof Completable)) {
231 |       return EMPTY_COMPLETION_RESULT;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     const def: CompletableDef<ZodString> = field._def;
235 |     const suggestions = await def.complete(request.params.argument.value);
236 |     return createCompletionResult(suggestions);
237 |   }
238 | 
239 |   private async handleResourceCompletion(
240 |     request: CompleteRequest,
241 |     ref: ResourceReference,
242 |   ): Promise<CompleteResult> {
243 |     const template = Object.values(this._registeredResourceTemplates).find(
244 |       (t) => t.resourceTemplate.uriTemplate.toString() === ref.uri,
245 |     );
246 | 
247 |     if (!template) {
248 |       if (this._registeredResources[ref.uri]) {
249 |         // Attempting to autocomplete a fixed resource URI is not an error in the spec (but probably should be).
250 |         return EMPTY_COMPLETION_RESULT;
251 |       }
252 | 
253 |       throw new McpError(
254 |         ErrorCode.InvalidParams,
255 |         `Resource template ${request.params.ref.uri} not found`,
256 |       );
257 |     }
258 | 
259 |     const completer = template.resourceTemplate.completeCallback(
260 |       request.params.argument.name,
261 |     );
262 |     if (!completer) {
263 |       return EMPTY_COMPLETION_RESULT;
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     const suggestions = await completer(request.params.argument.value);
267 |     return createCompletionResult(suggestions);
268 |   }
269 | 
270 |   private _resourceHandlersInitialized = false;
271 | 
272 |   private setResourceRequestHandlers() {
273 |     if (this._resourceHandlersInitialized) {
274 |       return;
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     this.server.assertCanSetRequestHandler(
278 |       ListResourcesRequestSchema.shape.method.value,
279 |     );
280 |     this.server.assertCanSetRequestHandler(
281 |       ListResourceTemplatesRequestSchema.shape.method.value,
282 |     );
283 |     this.server.assertCanSetRequestHandler(
284 |       ReadResourceRequestSchema.shape.method.value,
285 |     );
286 | 
287 |     this.server.registerCapabilities({
288 |       resources: {},
289 |     });
290 | 
291 |     this.server.setRequestHandler(
292 |       ListResourcesRequestSchema,
293 |       async (request, extra) => {
294 |         const resources = Object.entries(this._registeredResources).map(
295 |           ([uri, resource]) => ({
296 |             uri,
297 |             name: resource.name,
298 |             ...resource.metadata,
299 |           }),
300 |         );
301 | 
302 |         const templateResources: Resource[] = [];
303 |         for (const template of Object.values(
304 |           this._registeredResourceTemplates,
305 |         )) {
306 |           if (!template.resourceTemplate.listCallback) {
307 |             continue;
308 |           }
309 | 
310 |           const result = await template.resourceTemplate.listCallback(extra);
311 |           for (const resource of result.resources) {
312 |             templateResources.push({
313 |               ...resource,
314 |               ...template.metadata,
315 |             });
316 |           }
317 |         }
318 | 
319 |         return { resources: [...resources, ...templateResources] };
320 |       },
321 |     );
322 | 
323 |     this.server.setRequestHandler(
324 |       ListResourceTemplatesRequestSchema,
325 |       async () => {
326 |         const resourceTemplates = Object.entries(
327 |           this._registeredResourceTemplates,
328 |         ).map(([name, template]) => ({
329 |           name,
330 |           uriTemplate: template.resourceTemplate.uriTemplate.toString(),
331 |           ...template.metadata,
332 |         }));
333 | 
334 |         return { resourceTemplates };
335 |       },
336 |     );
337 | 
338 |     this.server.setRequestHandler(
339 |       ReadResourceRequestSchema,
340 |       async (request, extra) => {
341 |         const uri = new URL(request.params.uri);
342 | 
343 |         // First check for exact resource match
344 |         const resource = this._registeredResources[uri.toString()];
345 |         if (resource) {
346 |           return resource.readCallback(uri, extra);
347 |         }
348 | 
349 |         // Then check templates
350 |         for (const template of Object.values(
351 |           this._registeredResourceTemplates,
352 |         )) {
353 |           const variables = template.resourceTemplate.uriTemplate.match(
354 |             uri.toString(),
355 |           );
356 |           if (variables) {
357 |             return template.readCallback(uri, variables, extra);
358 |           }
359 |         }
360 | 
361 |         throw new McpError(
362 |           ErrorCode.InvalidParams,
363 |           `Resource ${uri} not found`,
364 |         );
365 |       },
366 |     );
367 | 
368 |     this.setCompletionRequestHandler();
369 |     
370 |     this._resourceHandlersInitialized = true;
371 |   }
372 |

445 |   /**
446 |    * Registers a resource `name` at a fixed URI, which will use the given callback to respond to read requests.
447 |    */
448 |   resource(name: string, uri: string, readCallback: ReadResourceCallback): void;
449 | 
450 |   /**
451 |    * Registers a resource `name` at a fixed URI with metadata, which will use the given callback to respond to read requests.
452 |    */
453 |   resource(
454 |     name: string,
455 |     uri: string,
456 |     metadata: ResourceMetadata,
457 |     readCallback: ReadResourceCallback,
458 |   ): void;
459 | 
460 |   /**
461 |    * Registers a resource `name` with a template pattern, which will use the given callback to respond to read requests.
462 |    */
463 |   resource(
464 |     name: string,
465 |     template: ResourceTemplate,
466 |     readCallback: ReadResourceTemplateCallback,
467 |   ): void;
468 | 
469 |   /**
470 |    * Registers a resource `name` with a template pattern and metadata, which will use the given callback to respond to read requests.
471 |    */
472 |   resource(
473 |     name: string,
474 |     template: ResourceTemplate,
475 |     metadata: ResourceMetadata,
476 |     readCallback: ReadResourceTemplateCallback,
477 |   ): void;
478 | 
479 |   resource(
480 |     name: string,
481 |     uriOrTemplate: string | ResourceTemplate,
482 |     ...rest: unknown[]
483 |   ): void {
484 |     let metadata: ResourceMetadata | undefined;
485 |     if (typeof rest[0] === "object") {
486 |       metadata = rest.shift() as ResourceMetadata;
487 |     }
488 | 
489 |     const readCallback = rest[0] as
490 |       | ReadResourceCallback
491 |       | ReadResourceTemplateCallback;
492 | 
493 |     if (typeof uriOrTemplate === "string") {
494 |       if (this._registeredResources[uriOrTemplate]) {
495 |         throw new Error(`Resource ${uriOrTemplate} is already registered`);
496 |       }
497 | 
498 |       this._registeredResources[uriOrTemplate] = {
499 |         name,
500 |         metadata,
501 |         readCallback: readCallback as ReadResourceCallback,
502 |       };
503 |     } else {
504 |       if (this._registeredResourceTemplates[name]) {
505 |         throw new Error(`Resource template ${name} is already registered`);
506 |       }
507 | 
508 |       this._registeredResourceTemplates[name] = {
509 |         resourceTemplate: uriOrTemplate,
510 |         metadata,
511 |         readCallback: readCallback as ReadResourceTemplateCallback,
512 |       };
513 |     }
514 | 
515 |     this.setResourceRequestHandlers();
516 |   }
517 | 
518 |   /**
519 |    * Registers a zero-argument tool `name`, which will run the given function when the client calls it.
520 |    */
521 |   tool(name: string, cb: ToolCallback): void;
522 | 
523 |   /**
524 |    * Registers a zero-argument tool `name` (with a description) which will run the given function when the client calls it.
525 |    */
526 |   tool(name: string, description: string, cb: ToolCallback): void;
527 | 
528 |   /**
529 |    * Registers a tool `name` accepting the given arguments, which must be an object containing named properties associated with Zod schemas. When the client calls it, the function will be run with the parsed and validated arguments.
530 |    */
531 |   tool<Args extends ZodRawShape>(
532 |     name: string,
533 |     paramsSchema: Args,
534 |     cb: ToolCallback<Args>,
535 |   ): void;
536 | 
537 |   /**
538 |    * Registers a tool `name` (with a description) accepting the given arguments, which must be an object containing named properties associated with Zod schemas. When the client calls it, the function will be run with the parsed and validated arguments.
539 |    */
540 |   tool<Args extends ZodRawShape>(
541 |     name: string,
542 |     description: string,
543 |     paramsSchema: Args,
544 |     cb: ToolCallback<Args>,
545 |   ): void;
546 | 
547 |   tool(name: string, ...rest: unknown[]): void {
548 |     if (this._registeredTools[name]) {
549 |       throw new Error(`Tool ${name} is already registered`);
550 |     }
551 | 
552 |     let description: string | undefined;
553 |     if (typeof rest[0] === "string") {
554 |       description = rest.shift() as string;
555 |     }
556 | 
557 |     let paramsSchema: ZodRawShape | undefined;
558 |     if (rest.length > 1) {
559 |       paramsSchema = rest.shift() as ZodRawShape;
560 |     }
561 | 
562 |     const cb = rest[0] as ToolCallback<ZodRawShape | undefined>;
563 |     this._registeredTools[name] = {
564 |       description,
565 |       inputSchema:
566 |         paramsSchema === undefined ? undefined : z.object(paramsSchema),
567 |       callback: cb,
568 |     };
569 | 
570 |     this.setToolRequestHandlers();
571 |   }
572 | 
573 |   /**
574 |    * Registers a zero-argument prompt `name`, which will run the given function when the client calls it.
575 |    */
576 |   prompt(name: string, cb: PromptCallback): void;
577 | 
578 |   /**
579 |    * Registers a zero-argument prompt `name` (with a description) which will run the given function when the client calls it.
580 |    */
581 |   prompt(name: string, description: string, cb: PromptCallback): void;
582 | 
583 |   /**
584 |    * Registers a prompt `name` accepting the given arguments, which must be an object containing named properties associated with Zod schemas. When the client calls it, the function will be run with the parsed and validated arguments.
585 |    */
586 |   prompt<Args extends PromptArgsRawShape>(
587 |     name: string,
588 |     argsSchema: Args,
589 |     cb: PromptCallback<Args>,
590 |   ): void;
591 | 
592 |   /**
593 |    * Registers a prompt `name` (with a description) accepting the given arguments, which must be an object containing named properties associated with Zod schemas. When the client calls it, the function will be run with the parsed and validated arguments.
594 |    */
595 |   prompt<Args extends PromptArgsRawShape>(
596 |     name: string,
597 |     description: string,
598 |     argsSchema: Args,
599 |     cb: PromptCallback<Args>,
600 |   ): void;
601 | 
602 |   prompt(name: string, ...rest: unknown[]): void {
603 |     if (this._registeredPrompts[name]) {
604 |       throw new Error(`Prompt ${name} is already registered`);
605 |     }
606 | 
607 |     let description: string | undefined;
608 |     if (typeof rest[0] === "string") {
609 |       description = rest.shift() as string;
610 |     }
611 | 
612 |     let argsSchema: PromptArgsRawShape | undefined;
613 |     if (rest.length > 1) {
614 |       argsSchema = rest.shift() as PromptArgsRawShape;
615 |     }
616 | 
617 |     const cb = rest[0] as PromptCallback<PromptArgsRawShape | undefined>;
618 |     this._registeredPrompts[name] = {
619 |       description,
620 |       argsSchema: argsSchema === undefined ? undefined : z.object(argsSchema),
621 |       callback: cb,
622 |     };
623 | 
624 |     this.setPromptRequestHandlers();
625 |   }
626 | }

628 | /**
629 |  * A callback to complete one variable within a resource template's URI template.
630 |  */
631 | export type CompleteResourceTemplateCallback = (
632 |   value: string,
633 | ) => string[] | Promise<string[]>;
634 | 
635 | /**
636 |  * A resource template combines a URI pattern with optional functionality to enumerate
637 |  * all resources matching that pattern.
638 |  */
639 | export class ResourceTemplate {
640 |   private _uriTemplate: UriTemplate;
641 | 
642 |   constructor(
643 |     uriTemplate: string | UriTemplate,
644 |     private _callbacks: {
645 |       /**
646 |        * A callback to list all resources matching this template. This is required to specified, even if `undefined`, to avoid accidentally forgetting resource listing.
647 |        */
648 |       list: ListResourcesCallback | undefined;
649 | 
650 |       /**
651 |        * An optional callback to autocomplete variables within the URI template. Useful for clients and users to discover possible values.
652 |        */
653 |       complete?: {
654 |         [variable: string]: CompleteResourceTemplateCallback;
655 |       };
656 |     },
657 |   ) {
658 |     this._uriTemplate =
659 |       typeof uriTemplate === "string"
660 |         ? new UriTemplate(uriTemplate)
661 |         : uriTemplate;
662 |   }
663 | 
664 |   /**
665 |    * Gets the URI template pattern.
666 |    */
667 |   get uriTemplate(): UriTemplate {
668 |     return this._uriTemplate;
669 |   }
670 | 
671 |   /**
672 |    * Gets the list callback, if one was provided.
673 |    */
674 |   get listCallback(): ListResourcesCallback | undefined {
675 |     return this._callbacks.list;
676 |   }
677 | 
678 |   /**
679 |    * Gets the callback for completing a specific URI template variable, if one was provided.
680 |    */
681 |   completeCallback(
682 |     variable: string,
683 |   ): CompleteResourceTemplateCallback | undefined {
684 |     return this._callbacks.complete?.[variable];
685 |   }
686 | }
687 | 
688 | /**
689 |  * Callback for a tool handler registered with Server.tool().
690 |  *
691 |  * Parameters will include tool arguments, if applicable, as well as other request handler context.
692 |  */
693 | export type ToolCallback<Args extends undefined | ZodRawShape = undefined> =
694 |   Args extends ZodRawShape
695 |     ? (
696 |         args: z.objectOutputType<Args, ZodTypeAny>,
697 |         extra: RequestHandlerExtra,
698 |       ) => CallToolResult | Promise<CallToolResult>
699 |     : (extra: RequestHandlerExtra) => CallToolResult | Promise<CallToolResult>;
700 | 
701 | type RegisteredTool = {
702 |   description?: string;
703 |   inputSchema?: AnyZodObject;
704 |   callback: ToolCallback<undefined | ZodRawShape>;
705 | };
706 | 
707 | const EMPTY_OBJECT_JSON_SCHEMA = {
708 |   type: "object" as const,
709 | };
710 | 
711 | /**
712 |  * Additional, optional information for annotating a resource.
713 |  */
714 | export type ResourceMetadata = Omit<Resource, "uri" | "name">;
715 | 
716 | /**
717 |  * Callback to list all resources matching a given template.
718 |  */
719 | export type ListResourcesCallback = (
720 |   extra: RequestHandlerExtra,
721 | ) => ListResourcesResult | Promise<ListResourcesResult>;
722 | 
723 | /**
724 |  * Callback to read a resource at a given URI.
725 |  */
726 | export type ReadResourceCallback = (
727 |   uri: URL,
728 |   extra: RequestHandlerExtra,
729 | ) => ReadResourceResult | Promise<ReadResourceResult>;
730 | 
731 | type RegisteredResource = {
732 |   name: string;
733 |   metadata?: ResourceMetadata;
734 |   readCallback: ReadResourceCallback;
735 | };
736 | 
737 | /**
738 |  * Callback to read a resource at a given URI, following a filled-in URI template.
739 |  */
740 | export type ReadResourceTemplateCallback = (
741 |   uri: URL,
742 |   variables: Variables,
743 |   extra: RequestHandlerExtra,
744 | ) => ReadResourceResult | Promise<ReadResourceResult>;
745 | 
746 | type RegisteredResourceTemplate = {
747 |   resourceTemplate: ResourceTemplate;
748 |   metadata?: ResourceMetadata;
749 |   readCallback: ReadResourceTemplateCallback;
750 | };
751 | 
752 | type PromptArgsRawShape = {
753 |   [k: string]:
754 |     | ZodType<string, ZodTypeDef, string>
755 |     | ZodOptional<ZodType<string, ZodTypeDef, string>>;
756 | };
757 | 
758 | export type PromptCallback<
759 |   Args extends undefined | PromptArgsRawShape = undefined,
760 | > = Args extends PromptArgsRawShape
761 |   ? (
762 |       args: z.objectOutputType<Args, ZodTypeAny>,
763 |       extra: RequestHandlerExtra,
764 |     ) => GetPromptResult | Promise<GetPromptResult>
765 |   : (extra: RequestHandlerExtra) => GetPromptResult | Promise<GetPromptResult>;
766 | 
767 | type RegisteredPrompt = {
768 |   description?: string;
769 |   argsSchema?: ZodObject<PromptArgsRawShape>;
770 |   callback: PromptCallback<undefined | PromptArgsRawShape>;
771 | };
772 | 
773 | function promptArgumentsFromSchema(
774 |   schema: ZodObject<PromptArgsRawShape>,
775 | ): PromptArgument[] {
776 |   return Object.entries(schema.shape).map(
777 |     ([name, field]): PromptArgument => ({
778 |       name,
779 |       description: field.description,
780 |       required: !field.isOptional(),
781 |     }),
782 |   );
783 | }
784 | 
785 | function createCompletionResult(suggestions: string[]): CompleteResult {
786 |   return {
787 |     completion: {
788 |       values: suggestions.slice(0, 100),
789 |       total: suggestions.length,
790 |       hasMore: suggestions.length > 100,
791 |     },
792 |   };
793 | }
794 | 
795 | const EMPTY_COMPLETION_RESULT: CompleteResult = {
796 |   completion: {
797 |     values: [],
798 |     hasMore: false,
799 |   },
800 | };
801 |

/src/server/sse.ts:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1 | import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
  2 | import { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
  3 | import { Transport } from "../shared/transport.js";
  4 | import { JSONRPCMessage, JSONRPCMessageSchema } from "../types.js";
  5 | import getRawBody from "raw-body";
  6 | import contentType from "content-type";
  7 | 
  8 | const MAXIMUM_MESSAGE_SIZE = "4mb";
  9 | 
 10 | /**
 11 |  * Server transport for SSE: this will send messages over an SSE connection and receive messages from HTTP POST requests.
 12 |  *
 13 |  * This transport is only available in Node.js environments.
 14 |  */
 15 | export class SSEServerTransport implements Transport {
 16 |   private _sseResponse?: ServerResponse;
 17 |   private _sessionId: string;
 18 | 
 19 |   onclose?: () => void;
 20 |   onerror?: (error: Error) => void;
 21 |   onmessage?: (message: JSONRPCMessage) => void;
 22 | 
 23 |   /**
 24 |    * Creates a new SSE server transport, which will direct the client to POST messages to the relative or absolute URL identified by `_endpoint`.
 25 |    */
 26 |   constructor(
 27 |     private _endpoint: string,
 28 |     private res: ServerResponse,
 29 |   ) {
 30 |     this._sessionId = randomUUID();
 31 |   }
 32 | 
 33 |   /**
 34 |    * Handles the initial SSE connection request.
 35 |    *
 36 |    * This should be called when a GET request is made to establish the SSE stream.
 37 |    */
 38 |   async start(): Promise<void> {
 39 |     if (this._sseResponse) {
 40 |       throw new Error(
 41 |         "SSEServerTransport already started! If using Server class, note that connect() calls start() automatically.",
 42 |       );
 43 |     }
 44 | 
 45 |     this.res.writeHead(200, {
 46 |       "Content-Type": "text/event-stream",
 47 |       "Cache-Control": "no-cache",
 48 |       Connection: "keep-alive",
 49 |     });
 50 | 
 51 |     // Send the endpoint event
 52 |     this.res.write(
 53 |       `event: endpoint\ndata: ${encodeURI(this._endpoint)}?sessionId=${this._sessionId}\n\n`,
 54 |     );
 55 | 
 56 |     this._sseResponse = this.res;
 57 |     this.res.on("close", () => {
 58 |       this._sseResponse = undefined;
 59 |       this.onclose?.();
 60 |     });
 61 |   }
 62 | 
 63 |   /**
 64 |    * Handles incoming POST messages.
 65 |    *
 66 |    * This should be called when a POST request is made to send a message to the server.
 67 |    */
 68 |   async handlePostMessage(
 69 |     req: IncomingMessage,
 70 |     res: ServerResponse,
 71 |     parsedBody?: unknown,
 72 |   ): Promise<void> {
 73 |     if (!this._sseResponse) {
 74 |       const message = "SSE connection not established";
 75 |       res.writeHead(500).end(message);
 76 |       throw new Error(message);
 77 |     }
 78 | 
 79 |     let body: string | unknown;
 80 |     try {
 81 |       const ct = contentType.parse(req.headers["content-type"] ?? "");
 82 |       if (ct.type !== "application/json") {
 83 |         throw new Error(`Unsupported content-type: ${ct}`);
 84 |       }
 85 | 
 86 |       body = parsedBody ?? await getRawBody(req, {
 87 |         limit: MAXIMUM_MESSAGE_SIZE,
 88 |         encoding: ct.parameters.charset ?? "utf-8",
 89 |       });
 90 |     } catch (error) {
 91 |       res.writeHead(400).end(String(error));
 92 |       this.onerror?.(error as Error);
 93 |       return;
 94 |     }
 95 | 
 96 |     try {
 97 |       await this.handleMessage(typeof body === 'string' ? JSON.parse(body) : body);
 98 |     } catch {
 99 |       res.writeHead(400).end(`Invalid message: ${body}`);
100 |       return;
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     res.writeHead(202).end("Accepted");
104 |   }
105 | 
106 |   /**
107 |    * Handle a client message, regardless of how it arrived. This can be used to inform the server of messages that arrive via a means different than HTTP POST.
108 |    */
109 |   async handleMessage(message: unknown): Promise<void> {
110 |     let parsedMessage: JSONRPCMessage;
111 |     try {
112 |       parsedMessage = JSONRPCMessageSchema.parse(message);
113 |     } catch (error) {
114 |       this.onerror?.(error as Error);
115 |       throw error;
116 |     }
117 | 
118 |     this.onmessage?.(parsedMessage);
119 |   }
120 | 
121 |   async close(): Promise<void> {
122 |     this._sseResponse?.end();
123 |     this._sseResponse = undefined;
124 |     this.onclose?.();
125 |   }
126 | 
127 |   async send(message: JSONRPCMessage): Promise<void> {
128 |     if (!this._sseResponse) {
129 |       throw new Error("Not connected");
130 |     }
131 | 
132 |     this._sseResponse.write(
133 |       `event: message\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(message)}\n\n`,
134 |     );
135 |   }
136 | 
137 |   /**
138 |    * Returns the session ID for this transport.
139 |    *
140 |    * This can be used to route incoming POST requests.
141 |    */
142 |   get sessionId(): string {
143 |     return this._sessionId;
144 |   }
145 | }
146 |

/src/server/stdio.test.ts:

  1 | import { Readable, Writable } from "node:stream";
  2 | import { ReadBuffer, serializeMessage } from "../shared/stdio.js";
  3 | import { JSONRPCMessage } from "../types.js";
  4 | import { StdioServerTransport } from "./stdio.js";
  5 | 
  6 | let input: Readable;
  7 | let outputBuffer: ReadBuffer;
  8 | let output: Writable;
  9 | 
 10 | beforeEach(() => {
 11 |   input = new Readable({
 12 |     // We'll use input.push() instead.
 13 |     read: () => {},
 14 |   });
 15 | 
 16 |   outputBuffer = new ReadBuffer();
 17 |   output = new Writable({
 18 |     write(chunk, encoding, callback) {
 19 |       outputBuffer.append(chunk);
 20 |       callback();
 21 |     },
 22 |   });
 23 | });
 24 | 
 25 | test("should start then close cleanly", async () => {
 26 |   const server = new StdioServerTransport(input, output);
 27 |   server.onerror = (error) => {
 28 |     throw error;
 29 |   };
 30 | 
 31 |   let didClose = false;
 32 |   server.onclose = () => {
 33 |     didClose = true;
 34 |   };
 35 | 
 36 |   await server.start();
 37 |   expect(didClose).toBeFalsy();
 38 |   await server.close();
 39 |   expect(didClose).toBeTruthy();
 40 | });
 41 | 
 42 | test("should not read until started", async () => {
 43 |   const server = new StdioServerTransport(input, output);
 44 |   server.onerror = (error) => {
 45 |     throw error;
 46 |   };
 47 | 
 48 |   let didRead = false;
 49 |   const readMessage = new Promise((resolve) => {
 50 |     server.onmessage = (message) => {
 51 |       didRead = true;
 52 |       resolve(message);
 53 |     };
 54 |   });
 55 | 
 56 |   const message: JSONRPCMessage = {
 57 |     jsonrpc: "2.0",
 58 |     id: 1,
 59 |     method: "ping",
 60 |   };
 61 |   input.push(serializeMessage(message));
 62 | 
 63 |   expect(didRead).toBeFalsy();
 64 |   await server.start();
 65 |   expect(await readMessage).toEqual(message);
 66 | });
 67 | 
 68 | test("should read multiple messages", async () => {
 69 |   const server = new StdioServerTransport(input, output);
 70 |   server.onerror = (error) => {
 71 |     throw error;
 72 |   };
 73 | 
 74 |   const messages: JSONRPCMessage[] = [
 75 |     {
 76 |       jsonrpc: "2.0",
 77 |       id: 1,
 78 |       method: "ping",
 79 |     },
 80 |     {
 81 |       jsonrpc: "2.0",
 82 |       method: "notifications/initialized",
 83 |     },
 84 |   ];
 85 | 
 86 |   const readMessages: JSONRPCMessage[] = [];
 87 |   const finished = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
 88 |     server.onmessage = (message) => {
 89 |       readMessages.push(message);
 90 |       if (JSON.stringify(message) === JSON.stringify(messages[1])) {
 91 |         resolve();
 92 |       }
 93 |     };
 94 |   });
 95 | 
 96 |   input.push(serializeMessage(messages[0]));
 97 |   input.push(serializeMessage(messages[1]));
 98 | 
 99 |   await server.start();
100 |   await finished;
101 |   expect(readMessages).toEqual(messages);
102 | });
103 |

/src/server/stdio.ts:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 1 | import process from "node:process";
 2 | import { Readable, Writable } from "node:stream";
 3 | import { ReadBuffer, serializeMessage } from "../shared/stdio.js";
 4 | import { JSONRPCMessage } from "../types.js";
 5 | import { Transport } from "../shared/transport.js";
 6 | 
 7 | /**
 8 |  * Server transport for stdio: this communicates with a MCP client by reading from the current process' stdin and writing to stdout.
 9 |  *
10 |  * This transport is only available in Node.js environments.
11 |  */
12 | export class StdioServerTransport implements Transport {
13 |   private _readBuffer: ReadBuffer = new ReadBuffer();
14 |   private _started = false;
15 | 
16 |   constructor(
17 |     private _stdin: Readable = process.stdin,
18 |     private _stdout: Writable = process.stdout,
19 |   ) {}
20 | 
21 |   onclose?: () => void;
22 |   onerror?: (error: Error) => void;
23 |   onmessage?: (message: JSONRPCMessage) => void;
24 | 
25 |   // Arrow functions to bind `this` properly, while maintaining function identity.
26 |   _ondata = (chunk: Buffer) => {
27 |     this._readBuffer.append(chunk);
28 |     this.processReadBuffer();
29 |   };
30 |   _onerror = (error: Error) => {
31 |     this.onerror?.(error);
32 |   };
33 | 
34 |   /**
35 |    * Starts listening for messages on stdin.
36 |    */
37 |   async start(): Promise<void> {
38 |     if (this._started) {
39 |       throw new Error(
40 |         "StdioServerTransport already started! If using Server class, note that connect() calls start() automatically.",
41 |       );
42 |     }
43 | 
44 |     this._started = true;
45 |     this._stdin.on("data", this._ondata);
46 |     this._stdin.on("error", this._onerror);
47 |   }
48 | 
49 |   private processReadBuffer() {
50 |     while (true) {
51 |       try {
52 |         const message = this._readBuffer.readMessage();
53 |         if (message === null) {
54 |           break;
55 |         }
56 | 
57 |         this.onmessage?.(message);
58 |       } catch (error) {
59 |         this.onerror?.(error as Error);
60 |       }
61 |     }
62 |   }
63 | 
64 |   async close(): Promise<void> {
65 |     // Remove our event listeners first
66 |     this._stdin.off("data", this._ondata);
67 |     this._stdin.off("error", this._onerror);
68 | 
69 |     // Check if we were the only data listener
70 |     const remainingDataListeners = this._stdin.listenerCount('data');
71 |     if (remainingDataListeners === 0) {
72 |       // Only pause stdin if we were the only listener
73 |       // This prevents interfering with other parts of the application that might be using stdin
74 |       this._stdin.pause();
75 |     }
76 |     
77 |     // Clear the buffer and notify closure
78 |     this._readBuffer.clear();
79 |     this.onclose?.();
80 |   }
81 | 
82 |   send(message: JSONRPCMessage): Promise<void> {
83 |     return new Promise((resolve) => {
84 |       const json = serializeMessage(message);
85 |       if (this._stdout.write(json)) {
86 |         resolve();
87 |       } else {
88 |         this._stdout.once("drain", resolve);
89 |       }
90 |     });
91 |   }
92 | }
93 |

/src/shared/auth.ts:

  1 | import { z } from "zod";
  2 | 
  3 | /**
  4 |  * RFC 8414 OAuth 2.0 Authorization Server Metadata
  5 |  */
  6 | export const OAuthMetadataSchema = z
  7 |   .object({
  8 |     issuer: z.string(),
  9 |     authorization_endpoint: z.string(),
 10 |     token_endpoint: z.string(),
 11 |     registration_endpoint: z.string().optional(),
 12 |     scopes_supported: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
 13 |     response_types_supported: z.array(z.string()),
 14 |     response_modes_supported: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
 15 |     grant_types_supported: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
 16 |     token_endpoint_auth_methods_supported: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
 17 |     token_endpoint_auth_signing_alg_values_supported: z
 18 |       .array(z.string())
 19 |       .optional(),
 20 |     service_documentation: z.string().optional(),
 21 |     revocation_endpoint: z.string().optional(),
 22 |     revocation_endpoint_auth_methods_supported: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
 23 |     revocation_endpoint_auth_signing_alg_values_supported: z
 24 |       .array(z.string())
 25 |       .optional(),
 26 |     introspection_endpoint: z.string().optional(),
 27 |     introspection_endpoint_auth_methods_supported: z
 28 |       .array(z.string())
 29 |       .optional(),
 30 |     introspection_endpoint_auth_signing_alg_values_supported: z
 31 |       .array(z.string())
 32 |       .optional(),
 33 |     code_challenge_methods_supported: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
 34 |   })
 35 |   .passthrough();
 36 | 
 37 | /**
 38 |  * OAuth 2.1 token response
 39 |  */
 40 | export const OAuthTokensSchema = z
 41 |   .object({
 42 |     access_token: z.string(),
 43 |     token_type: z.string(),
 44 |     expires_in: z.number().optional(),
 45 |     scope: z.string().optional(),
 46 |     refresh_token: z.string().optional(),
 47 |   })
 48 |   .strip();
 49 | 
 50 | /**
 51 |  * OAuth 2.1 error response
 52 |  */
 53 | export const OAuthErrorResponseSchema = z
 54 |   .object({
 55 |     error: z.string(),
 56 |     error_description: z.string().optional(),
 57 |     error_uri: z.string().optional(),
 58 |   });
 59 | 
 60 | /**
 61 |  * RFC 7591 OAuth 2.0 Dynamic Client Registration metadata
 62 |  */
 63 | export const OAuthClientMetadataSchema = z.object({
 64 |   redirect_uris: z.array(z.string()).refine((uris) => uris.every((uri) => URL.canParse(uri)), { message: "redirect_uris must contain valid URLs" }),
 65 |   token_endpoint_auth_method: z.string().optional(),
 66 |   grant_types: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
 67 |   response_types: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
 68 |   client_name: z.string().optional(),
 69 |   client_uri: z.string().optional(),
 70 |   logo_uri: z.string().optional(),
 71 |   scope: z.string().optional(),
 72 |   contacts: z.array(z.string()).optional(),
 73 |   tos_uri: z.string().optional(),
 74 |   policy_uri: z.string().optional(),
 75 |   jwks_uri: z.string().optional(),
 76 |   jwks: z.any().optional(),
 77 |   software_id: z.string().optional(),
 78 |   software_version: z.string().optional(),
 79 | }).strip();
 80 | 
 81 | /**
 82 |  * RFC 7591 OAuth 2.0 Dynamic Client Registration client information
 83 |  */
 84 | export const OAuthClientInformationSchema = z.object({
 85 |   client_id: z.string(),
 86 |   client_secret: z.string().optional(),
 87 |   client_id_issued_at: z.number().optional(),
 88 |   client_secret_expires_at: z.number().optional(),
 89 | }).strip();
 90 | 
 91 | /**
 92 |  * RFC 7591 OAuth 2.0 Dynamic Client Registration full response (client information plus metadata)
 93 |  */
 94 | export const OAuthClientInformationFullSchema = OAuthClientMetadataSchema.merge(OAuthClientInformationSchema);
 95 | 
 96 | /**
 97 |  * RFC 7591 OAuth 2.0 Dynamic Client Registration error response
 98 |  */
 99 | export const OAuthClientRegistrationErrorSchema = z.object({
100 |   error: z.string(),
101 |   error_description: z.string().optional(),
102 | }).strip();
103 | 
104 | /**
105 |  * RFC 7009 OAuth 2.0 Token Revocation request
106 |  */
107 | export const OAuthTokenRevocationRequestSchema = z.object({
108 |   token: z.string(),
109 |   token_type_hint: z.string().optional(),
110 | }).strip();
111 | 
112 | export type OAuthMetadata = z.infer<typeof OAuthMetadataSchema>;
113 | export type OAuthTokens = z.infer<typeof OAuthTokensSchema>;
114 | export type OAuthErrorResponse = z.infer<typeof OAuthErrorResponseSchema>;
115 | export type OAuthClientMetadata = z.infer<typeof OAuthClientMetadataSchema>;
116 | export type OAuthClientInformation = z.infer<typeof OAuthClientInformationSchema>;
117 | export type OAuthClientInformationFull = z.infer<typeof OAuthClientInformationFullSchema>;
118 | export type OAuthClientRegistrationError = z.infer<typeof OAuthClientRegistrationErrorSchema>;
119 | export type OAuthTokenRevocationRequest = z.infer<typeof OAuthTokenRevocationRequestSchema>;

/src/shared/protocol.test.ts:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1 | import { ZodType, z } from "zod";
  2 | import {
  3 |   ClientCapabilities,
  4 |   ErrorCode,
  5 |   McpError,
  6 |   Notification,
  7 |   Request,
  8 |   Result,
  9 |   ServerCapabilities,
 10 | } from "../types.js";
 11 | import { Protocol, mergeCapabilities } from "./protocol.js";
 12 | import { Transport } from "./transport.js";
 13 | 
 14 | // Mock Transport class
 15 | class MockTransport implements Transport {
 16 |   onclose?: () => void;
 17 |   onerror?: (error: Error) => void;
 18 |   onmessage?: (message: unknown) => void;
 19 | 
 20 |   async start(): Promise<void> {}
 21 |   async close(): Promise<void> {
 22 |     this.onclose?.();
 23 |   }
 24 |   async send(_message: unknown): Promise<void> {}
 25 | }
 26 | 
 27 | describe("protocol tests", () => {
 28 |   let protocol: Protocol<Request, Notification, Result>;
 29 |   let transport: MockTransport;
 30 | 
 31 |   beforeEach(() => {
 32 |     transport = new MockTransport();
 33 |     protocol = new (class extends Protocol<Request, Notification, Result> {
 34 |       protected assertCapabilityForMethod(): void {}
 35 |       protected assertNotificationCapability(): void {}
 36 |       protected assertRequestHandlerCapability(): void {}
 37 |     })();
 38 |   });
 39 | 
 40 |   test("should throw a timeout error if the request exceeds the timeout", async () => {
 41 |     await protocol.connect(transport);
 42 |     const request = { method: "example", params: {} };
 43 |     try {
 44 |       const mockSchema: ZodType<{ result: string }> = z.object({
 45 |         result: z.string(),
 46 |       });
 47 |       await protocol.request(request, mockSchema, {
 48 |         timeout: 0,
 49 |       });
 50 |     } catch (error) {
 51 |       expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(McpError);
 52 |       if (error instanceof McpError) {
 53 |         expect(error.code).toBe(ErrorCode.RequestTimeout);
 54 |       }
 55 |     }
 56 |   });
 57 | 
 58 |   test("should invoke onclose when the connection is closed", async () => {
 59 |     const oncloseMock = jest.fn();
 60 |     protocol.onclose = oncloseMock;
 61 |     await protocol.connect(transport);
 62 |     await transport.close();
 63 |     expect(oncloseMock).toHaveBeenCalled();
 64 |   });
 65 | 
 66 |   describe("progress notification timeout behavior", () => {
 67 |     beforeEach(() => {
 68 |       jest.useFakeTimers();
 69 |     });
 70 |     afterEach(() => {
 71 |       jest.useRealTimers();
 72 |     });
 73 | 
 74 |     test("should reset timeout when progress notification is received", async () => {
 75 |       await protocol.connect(transport);
 76 |       const request = { method: "example", params: {} };
 77 |       const mockSchema: ZodType<{ result: string }> = z.object({
 78 |         result: z.string(),
 79 |       });
 80 |       const onProgressMock = jest.fn();
 81 |       const requestPromise = protocol.request(request, mockSchema, {
 82 |         timeout: 1000,
 83 |         resetTimeoutOnProgress: true,
 84 |         onprogress: onProgressMock,
 85 |       });
 86 |       jest.advanceTimersByTime(800);
 87 |       if (transport.onmessage) {
 88 |         transport.onmessage({
 89 |           jsonrpc: "2.0",
 90 |           method: "notifications/progress",
 91 |           params: {
 92 |             progressToken: 0,
 93 |             progress: 50,
 94 |             total: 100,
 95 |           },
 96 |         });
 97 |       }
 98 |       await Promise.resolve();
 99 |       expect(onProgressMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
100 |         progress: 50,
101 |         total: 100,
102 |       });
103 |       jest.advanceTimersByTime(800);
104 |       if (transport.onmessage) {
105 |         transport.onmessage({
106 |           jsonrpc: "2.0",
107 |           id: 0,
108 |           result: { result: "success" },
109 |         });
110 |       }
111 |       await Promise.resolve();
112 |       await expect(requestPromise).resolves.toEqual({ result: "success" });
113 |     });
114 | 
115 |     test("should respect maxTotalTimeout", async () => {
116 |       await protocol.connect(transport);
117 |       const request = { method: "example", params: {} };
118 |       const mockSchema: ZodType<{ result: string }> = z.object({
119 |         result: z.string(),
120 |       });
121 |       const onProgressMock = jest.fn();
122 |       const requestPromise = protocol.request(request, mockSchema, {
123 |         timeout: 1000,
124 |         maxTotalTimeout: 150,
125 |         resetTimeoutOnProgress: true,
126 |         onprogress: onProgressMock,
127 |       });
128 | 
129 |       // First progress notification should work
130 |       jest.advanceTimersByTime(80);
131 |       if (transport.onmessage) {
132 |         transport.onmessage({
133 |           jsonrpc: "2.0",
134 |           method: "notifications/progress",
135 |           params: {
136 |             progressToken: 0,
137 |             progress: 50,
138 |             total: 100,
139 |           },
140 |         });
141 |       }
142 |       await Promise.resolve();
143 |       expect(onProgressMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
144 |         progress: 50,
145 |         total: 100,
146 |       });
147 |       jest.advanceTimersByTime(80);
148 |       if (transport.onmessage) {
149 |         transport.onmessage({
150 |           jsonrpc: "2.0",
151 |           method: "notifications/progress",
152 |           params: {
153 |             progressToken: 0,
154 |             progress: 75,
155 |             total: 100,
156 |           },
157 |         });
158 |       }
159 |       await expect(requestPromise).rejects.toThrow("Maximum total timeout exceeded");
160 |       expect(onProgressMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
161 |     });
162 | 
163 |     test("should timeout if no progress received within timeout period", async () => {
164 |       await protocol.connect(transport);
165 |       const request = { method: "example", params: {} };
166 |       const mockSchema: ZodType<{ result: string }> = z.object({
167 |         result: z.string(),
168 |       });
169 |       const requestPromise = protocol.request(request, mockSchema, {
170 |         timeout: 100,
171 |         resetTimeoutOnProgress: true,
172 |       });
173 |       jest.advanceTimersByTime(101);
174 |       await expect(requestPromise).rejects.toThrow("Request timed out");
175 |     });
176 | 
177 |     test("should handle multiple progress notifications correctly", async () => {
178 |       await protocol.connect(transport);
179 |       const request = { method: "example", params: {} };
180 |       const mockSchema: ZodType<{ result: string }> = z.object({
181 |         result: z.string(),
182 |       });
183 |       const onProgressMock = jest.fn();
184 |       const requestPromise = protocol.request(request, mockSchema, {
185 |         timeout: 1000,
186 |         resetTimeoutOnProgress: true,
187 |         onprogress: onProgressMock,
188 |       });
189 | 
190 |       // Simulate multiple progress updates
191 |       for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
192 |         jest.advanceTimersByTime(800);
193 |         if (transport.onmessage) {
194 |           transport.onmessage({
195 |             jsonrpc: "2.0",
196 |             method: "notifications/progress",
197 |             params: {
198 |               progressToken: 0,
199 |               progress: i * 25,
200 |               total: 100,
201 |             },
202 |           });
203 |         }
204 |         await Promise.resolve();
205 |         expect(onProgressMock).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(i, {
206 |           progress: i * 25,
207 |           total: 100,
208 |         });
209 |       }
210 |       if (transport.onmessage) {
211 |         transport.onmessage({
212 |           jsonrpc: "2.0",
213 |           id: 0,
214 |           result: { result: "success" },
215 |         });
216 |       }
217 |       await Promise.resolve();
218 |       await expect(requestPromise).resolves.toEqual({ result: "success" });
219 |     });
220 |   });
221 | });
222 | 
223 | describe("mergeCapabilities", () => {
224 |   it("should merge client capabilities", () => {
225 |     const base: ClientCapabilities = {
226 |       sampling: {},
227 |       roots: {
228 |         listChanged: true,
229 |       },
230 |     };
231 | 
232 |     const additional: ClientCapabilities = {
233 |       experimental: {
234 |         feature: true,
235 |       },
236 |       roots: {
237 |         newProp: true,
238 |       },
239 |     };
240 | 
241 |     const merged = mergeCapabilities(base, additional);
242 |     expect(merged).toEqual({
243 |       sampling: {},
244 |       roots: {
245 |         listChanged: true,
246 |         newProp: true,
247 |       },
248 |       experimental: {
249 |         feature: true,
250 |       },
251 |     });
252 |   });
253 | 
254 |   it("should merge server capabilities", () => {
255 |     const base: ServerCapabilities = {
256 |       logging: {},
257 |       prompts: {
258 |         listChanged: true,
259 |       },
260 |     };
261 | 
262 |     const additional: ServerCapabilities = {
263 |       resources: {
264 |         subscribe: true,
265 |       },
266 |       prompts: {
267 |         newProp: true,
268 |       },
269 |     };
270 | 
271 |     const merged = mergeCapabilities(base, additional);
272 |     expect(merged).toEqual({
273 |       logging: {},
274 |       prompts: {
275 |         listChanged: true,
276 |         newProp: true,
277 |       },
278 |       resources: {
279 |         subscribe: true,
280 |       },
281 |     });
282 |   });
283 | 
284 |   it("should override existing values with additional values", () => {
285 |     const base: ServerCapabilities = {
286 |       prompts: {
287 |         listChanged: false,
288 |       },
289 |     };
290 | 
291 |     const additional: ServerCapabilities = {
292 |       prompts: {
293 |         listChanged: true,
294 |       },
295 |     };
296 | 
297 |     const merged = mergeCapabilities(base, additional);
298 |     expect(merged.prompts!.listChanged).toBe(true);
299 |   });
300 | 
301 |   it("should handle empty objects", () => {
302 |     const base = {};
303 |     const additional = {};
304 |     const merged = mergeCapabilities(base, additional);
305 |     expect(merged).toEqual({});
306 |   });
307 | });

  1 | import { ZodLiteral, ZodObject, ZodType, z } from "zod";
  2 | import {
  3 |   CancelledNotificationSchema,
  4 |   ClientCapabilities,
  5 |   ErrorCode,
  6 |   JSONRPCError,
  7 |   JSONRPCNotification,
  8 |   JSONRPCRequest,
  9 |   JSONRPCResponse,
 10 |   McpError,
 11 |   Notification,
 12 |   PingRequestSchema,
 13 |   Progress,
 14 |   ProgressNotification,
 15 |   ProgressNotificationSchema,
 16 |   Request,
 17 |   RequestId,
 18 |   Result,
 19 |   ServerCapabilities,
 20 | } from "../types.js";
 21 | import { Transport } from "./transport.js";
 22 | 
 23 | /**
 24 |  * Callback for progress notifications.
 25 |  */
 26 | export type ProgressCallback = (progress: Progress) => void;
 27 | 
 28 | /**
 29 |  * Additional initialization options.
 30 |  */
 31 | export type ProtocolOptions = {
 32 |   /**
 33 |    * Whether to restrict emitted requests to only those that the remote side has indicated that they can handle, through their advertised capabilities.
 34 |    *
 35 |    * Note that this DOES NOT affect checking of _local_ side capabilities, as it is considered a logic error to mis-specify those.
 36 |    *
 37 |    * Currently this defaults to false, for backwards compatibility with SDK versions that did not advertise capabilities correctly. In future, this will default to true.
 38 |    */
 39 |   enforceStrictCapabilities?: boolean;
 40 | };
 41 | 
 42 | /**
 43 |  * The default request timeout, in miliseconds.
 44 |  */
 45 | export const DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MSEC = 60000;
 46 | 
 47 | /**
 48 |  * Options that can be given per request.
 49 |  */
 50 | export type RequestOptions = {
 51 |   /**
 52 |    * If set, requests progress notifications from the remote end (if supported). When progress notifications are received, this callback will be invoked.
 53 |    */
 54 |   onprogress?: ProgressCallback;
 55 | 
 56 |   /**
 57 |    * Can be used to cancel an in-flight request. This will cause an AbortError to be raised from request().
 58 |    */
 59 |   signal?: AbortSignal;
 60 | 
 61 |   /**
 62 |    * A timeout (in milliseconds) for this request. If exceeded, an McpError with code `RequestTimeout` will be raised from request().
 63 |    *
 64 |    * If not specified, `DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MSEC` will be used as the timeout.
 65 |    */
 66 |   timeout?: number;
 67 | 
 68 |   /**
 69 |    * If true, receiving a progress notification will reset the request timeout.
 70 |    * This is useful for long-running operations that send periodic progress updates.
 71 |    * Default: false
 72 |    */
 73 |   resetTimeoutOnProgress?: boolean;
 74 | 
 75 |   /**
 76 |    * Maximum total time (in milliseconds) to wait for a response.
 77 |    * If exceeded, an McpError with code `RequestTimeout` will be raised, regardless of progress notifications.
 78 |    * If not specified, there is no maximum total timeout.
 79 |    */
 80 |   maxTotalTimeout?: number;
 81 | };
 82 | 
 83 | /**
 84 |  * Extra data given to request handlers.
 85 |  */
 86 | export type RequestHandlerExtra = {
 87 |   /**
 88 |    * An abort signal used to communicate if the request was cancelled from the sender's side.
 89 |    */
 90 |   signal: AbortSignal;
 91 | 
 92 |   /**
 93 |    * The session ID from the transport, if available.
 94 |    */
 95 |   sessionId?: string;
 96 | };
 97 | 
 98 | /**
 99 |  * Information about a request's timeout state
100 |  */
101 | type TimeoutInfo = {
102 |   timeoutId: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
103 |   startTime: number;
104 |   timeout: number;
105 |   maxTotalTimeout?: number;
106 |   onTimeout: () => void;
107 | };
108 | 
109 | /**
110 |  * Implements MCP protocol framing on top of a pluggable transport, including
111 |  * features like request/response linking, notifications, and progress.
112 |  */
113 | export abstract class Protocol<
114 |   SendRequestT extends Request,
115 |   SendNotificationT extends Notification,
116 |   SendResultT extends Result,
117 | > {
118 |   private _transport?: Transport;
119 |   private _requestMessageId = 0;
120 |   private _requestHandlers: Map<
121 |     string,
122 |     (
123 |       request: JSONRPCRequest,
124 |       extra: RequestHandlerExtra,
125 |     ) => Promise<SendResultT>
126 |   > = new Map();
127 |   private _requestHandlerAbortControllers: Map<RequestId, AbortController> =
128 |     new Map();
129 |   private _notificationHandlers: Map<
130 |     string,
131 |     (notification: JSONRPCNotification) => Promise<void>
132 |   > = new Map();
133 |   private _responseHandlers: Map<
134 |     number,
135 |     (response: JSONRPCResponse | Error) => void
136 |   > = new Map();
137 |   private _progressHandlers: Map<number, ProgressCallback> = new Map();
138 |   private _timeoutInfo: Map<number, TimeoutInfo> = new Map();
139 | 
140 |   /**
141 |    * Callback for when the connection is closed for any reason.
142 |    *
143 |    * This is invoked when close() is called as well.
144 |    */
145 |   onclose?: () => void;
146 | 
147 |   /**
148 |    * Callback for when an error occurs.
149 |    *
150 |    * Note that errors are not necessarily fatal; they are used for reporting any kind of exceptional condition out of band.
151 |    */
152 |   onerror?: (error: Error) => void;
153 | 
154 |   /**
155 |    * A handler to invoke for any request types that do not have their own handler installed.
156 |    */
157 |   fallbackRequestHandler?: (request: Request) => Promise<SendResultT>;
158 | 
159 |   /**
160 |    * A handler to invoke for any notification types that do not have their own handler installed.
161 |    */
162 |   fallbackNotificationHandler?: (notification: Notification) => Promise<void>;
163 | 
164 |   constructor(private _options?: ProtocolOptions) {
165 |     this.setNotificationHandler(CancelledNotificationSchema, (notification) => {
166 |       const controller = this._requestHandlerAbortControllers.get(
167 |         notification.params.requestId,
168 |       );
169 |       controller?.abort(notification.params.reason);
170 |     });
171 | 
172 |     this.setNotificationHandler(ProgressNotificationSchema, (notification) => {
173 |       this._onprogress(notification as unknown as ProgressNotification);
174 |     });
175 | 
176 |     this.setRequestHandler(
177 |       PingRequestSchema,
178 |       // Automatic pong by default.
179 |       (_request) => ({}) as SendResultT,
180 |     );
181 |   }
182 | 
183 |   private _setupTimeout(
184 |     messageId: number,
185 |     timeout: number,
186 |     maxTotalTimeout: number | undefined,
187 |     onTimeout: () => void
188 |   ) {
189 |     this._timeoutInfo.set(messageId, {
190 |       timeoutId: setTimeout(onTimeout, timeout),
191 |       startTime: Date.now(),
192 |       timeout,
193 |       maxTotalTimeout,
194 |       onTimeout
195 |     });
196 |   }
197 | 
198 |   private _resetTimeout(messageId: number): boolean {
199 |     const info = this._timeoutInfo.get(messageId);
200 |     if (!info) return false;
201 | 
202 |     const totalElapsed = Date.now() - info.startTime;
203 |     if (info.maxTotalTimeout && totalElapsed >= info.maxTotalTimeout) {
204 |       this._timeoutInfo.delete(messageId);
205 |       throw new McpError(
206 |         ErrorCode.RequestTimeout,
207 |         "Maximum total timeout exceeded",
208 |         { maxTotalTimeout: info.maxTotalTimeout, totalElapsed }
209 |       );
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     clearTimeout(info.timeoutId);
213 |     info.timeoutId = setTimeout(info.onTimeout, info.timeout);
214 |     return true;
215 |   }
216 | 
217 |   private _cleanupTimeout(messageId: number) {
218 |     const info = this._timeoutInfo.get(messageId);
219 |     if (info) {
220 |       clearTimeout(info.timeoutId);
221 |       this._timeoutInfo.delete(messageId);
222 |     }
223 |   }
224 | 
225 |   /**
226 |    * Attaches to the given transport, starts it, and starts listening for messages.
227 |    *
228 |    * The Protocol object assumes ownership of the Transport, replacing any callbacks that have already been set, and expects that it is the only user of the Transport instance going forward.
229 |    */
230 |   async connect(transport: Transport): Promise<void> {
231 |     this._transport = transport;
232 |     this._transport.onclose = () => {
233 |       this._onclose();
234 |     };
235 | 
236 |     this._transport.onerror = (error: Error) => {
237 |       this._onerror(error);
238 |     };
239 | 
240 |     this._transport.onmessage = (message) => {
241 |       if (!("method" in message)) {
242 |         this._onresponse(message);
243 |       } else if ("id" in message) {
244 |         this._onrequest(message);
245 |       } else {
246 |         this._onnotification(message);
247 |       }
248 |     };
249 | 
250 |     await this._transport.start();
251 |   }
252 | 
253 |   private _onclose(): void {
254 |     const responseHandlers = this._responseHandlers;
255 |     this._responseHandlers = new Map();
256 |     this._progressHandlers.clear();
257 |     this._transport = undefined;
258 |     this.onclose?.();
259 | 
260 |     const error = new McpError(ErrorCode.ConnectionClosed, "Connection closed");
261 |     for (const handler of responseHandlers.values()) {
262 |       handler(error);
263 |     }
264 |   }
265 | 
266 |   private _onerror(error: Error): void {
267 |     this.onerror?.(error);
268 |   }
269 | 
270 |   private _onnotification(notification: JSONRPCNotification): void {
271 |     const handler =
272 |       this._notificationHandlers.get(notification.method) ??
273 |       this.fallbackNotificationHandler;
274 | 
275 |     // Ignore notifications not being subscribed to.
276 |     if (handler === undefined) {
277 |       return;
278 |     }
279 | 
280 |     // Starting with Promise.resolve() puts any synchronous errors into the monad as well.
281 |     Promise.resolve()
282 |       .then(() => handler(notification))
283 |       .catch((error) =>
284 |         this._onerror(
285 |           new Error(`Uncaught error in notification handler: ${error}`),
286 |         ),
287 |       );
288 |   }
289 | 
290 |   private _onrequest(request: JSONRPCRequest): void {
291 |     const handler =
292 |       this._requestHandlers.get(request.method) ?? this.fallbackRequestHandler;
293 | 
294 |     if (handler === undefined) {
295 |       this._transport
296 |         ?.send({
297 |           jsonrpc: "2.0",
298 |           id: request.id,
299 |           error: {
300 |             code: ErrorCode.MethodNotFound,
301 |             message: "Method not found",
302 |           },
303 |         })
304 |         .catch((error) =>
305 |           this._onerror(
306 |             new Error(`Failed to send an error response: ${error}`),
307 |           ),
308 |         );
309 |       return;
310 |     }
311 | 
312 |     const abortController = new AbortController();
313 |     this._requestHandlerAbortControllers.set(request.id, abortController);
314 | 
315 |     // Create extra object with both abort signal and sessionId from transport
316 |     const extra: RequestHandlerExtra = {
317 |       signal: abortController.signal,
318 |       sessionId: this._transport?.sessionId,
319 |     };
320 | 
321 |     // Starting with Promise.resolve() puts any synchronous errors into the monad as well.
322 |     Promise.resolve()
323 |       .then(() => handler(request, extra))
324 |       .then(
325 |         (result) => {
326 |           if (abortController.signal.aborted) {
327 |             return;
328 |           }
329 | 
330 |           return this._transport?.send({
331 |             result,
332 |             jsonrpc: "2.0",
333 |             id: request.id,
334 |           });
335 |         },
336 |         (error) => {
337 |           if (abortController.signal.aborted) {
338 |             return;
339 |           }
340 | 
341 |           return this._transport?.send({
342 |             jsonrpc: "2.0",
343 |             id: request.id,
344 |             error: {
345 |               code: Number.isSafeInteger(error["code"])
346 |                 ? error["code"]
347 |                 : ErrorCode.InternalError,
348 |               message: error.message ?? "Internal error",
349 |             },
350 |           });
351 |         },
352 |       )
353 |       .catch((error) =>
354 |         this._onerror(new Error(`Failed to send response: ${error}`)),
355 |       )
356 |       .finally(() => {
357 |         this._requestHandlerAbortControllers.delete(request.id);
358 |       });
359 |   }
360 | 
361 |   private _onprogress(notification: ProgressNotification): void {
362 |     const { progressToken, ...params } = notification.params;
363 |     const messageId = Number(progressToken);
364 |     
365 |     const handler = this._progressHandlers.get(messageId);
366 |     if (!handler) {
367 |       this._onerror(new Error(`Received a progress notification for an unknown token: ${JSON.stringify(notification)}`));
368 |       return;
369 |     }
370 | 
371 |     const responseHandler = this._responseHandlers.get(messageId);
372 |     if (this._timeoutInfo.has(messageId) && responseHandler) {
373 |       try {
374 |         this._resetTimeout(messageId);
375 |       } catch (error) {
376 |         responseHandler(error as Error);
377 |         return;
378 |       }
379 |     }
380 | 
381 |     handler(params);
382 |   }
383 | 
384 |   private _onresponse(response: JSONRPCResponse | JSONRPCError): void {
385 |     const messageId = Number(response.id);
386 |     const handler = this._responseHandlers.get(messageId);
387 |     if (handler === undefined) {
388 |       this._onerror(
389 |         new Error(
390 |           `Received a response for an unknown message ID: ${JSON.stringify(response)}`,
391 |         ),
392 |       );
393 |       return;
394 |     }
395 | 
396 |     this._responseHandlers.delete(messageId);
397 |     this._progressHandlers.delete(messageId);
398 |     this._cleanupTimeout(messageId);
399 | 
400 |     if ("result" in response) {
401 |       handler(response);
402 |     } else {
403 |       const error = new McpError(
404 |         response.error.code,
405 |         response.error.message,
406 |         response.error.data,
407 |       );
408 |       handler(error);
409 |     }
410 |   }
411 | 
412 |   get transport(): Transport | undefined {
413 |     return this._transport;
414 |   }
415 | 
416 |   /**
417 |    * Closes the connection.
418 |    */
419 |   async close(): Promise<void> {
420 |     await this._transport?.close();
421 |   }
422 | 
423 |   /**
424 |    * A method to check if a capability is supported by the remote side, for the given method to be called.
425 |    *
426 |    * This should be implemented by subclasses.
427 |    */
428 |   protected abstract assertCapabilityForMethod(
429 |     method: SendRequestT["method"],
430 |   ): void;
431 | 
432 |   /**
433 |    * A method to check if a notification is supported by the local side, for the given method to be sent.
434 |    *
435 |    * This should be implemented by subclasses.
436 |    */
437 |   protected abstract assertNotificationCapability(
438 |     method: SendNotificationT["method"],
439 |   ): void;
440 | 
441 |   /**
442 |    * A method to check if a request handler is supported by the local side, for the given method to be handled.
443 |    *
444 |    * This should be implemented by subclasses.
445 |    */
446 |   protected abstract assertRequestHandlerCapability(method: string): void;
447 | 
448 |   /**
449 |    * Sends a request and wait for a response.
450 |    *
451 |    * Do not use this method to emit notifications! Use notification() instead.
452 |    */
453 |   request<T extends ZodType<object>>(
454 |     request: SendRequestT,
455 |     resultSchema: T,
456 |     options?: RequestOptions,
457 |   ): Promise<z.infer<T>> {
458 |     return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
459 |       if (!this._transport) {
460 |         reject(new Error("Not connected"));
461 |         return;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       if (this._options?.enforceStrictCapabilities === true) {
465 |         this.assertCapabilityForMethod(request.method);
466 |       }
467 | 
468 |       options?.signal?.throwIfAborted();
469 | 
470 |       const messageId = this._requestMessageId++;
471 |       const jsonrpcRequest: JSONRPCRequest = {
472 |         ...request,
473 |         jsonrpc: "2.0",
474 |         id: messageId,
475 |       };
476 | 
477 |       if (options?.onprogress) {
478 |         this._progressHandlers.set(messageId, options.onprogress);
479 |         jsonrpcRequest.params = {
480 |           ...request.params,
481 |           _meta: { progressToken: messageId },
482 |         };
483 |       }
484 | 
485 |       const cancel = (reason: unknown) => {
486 |         this._responseHandlers.delete(messageId);
487 |         this._progressHandlers.delete(messageId);
488 |         this._cleanupTimeout(messageId);
489 | 
490 |         this._transport
491 |           ?.send({
492 |             jsonrpc: "2.0",
493 |             method: "notifications/cancelled",
494 |             params: {
495 |               requestId: messageId,
496 |               reason: String(reason),
497 |             },
498 |           })
499 |           .catch((error) =>
500 |             this._onerror(new Error(`Failed to send cancellation: ${error}`)),
501 |           );
502 | 
503 |         reject(reason);
504 |       };
505 | 
506 |       this._responseHandlers.set(messageId, (response) => {
507 |         if (options?.signal?.aborted) {
508 |           return;
509 |         }
510 | 
511 |         if (response instanceof Error) {
512 |           return reject(response);
513 |         }
514 | 
515 |         try {
516 |           const result = resultSchema.parse(response.result);
517 |           resolve(result);
518 |         } catch (error) {
519 |           reject(error);
520 |         }
521 |       });
522 | 
523 |       options?.signal?.addEventListener("abort", () => {
524 |         cancel(options?.signal?.reason);
525 |       });
526 | 
527 |       const timeout = options?.timeout ?? DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MSEC;
528 |       const timeoutHandler = () => cancel(new McpError(
529 |         ErrorCode.RequestTimeout,
530 |         "Request timed out",
531 |         { timeout }
532 |       ));
533 | 
534 |       this._setupTimeout(messageId, timeout, options?.maxTotalTimeout, timeoutHandler);
535 | 
536 |       this._transport.send(jsonrpcRequest).catch((error) => {
537 |         this._cleanupTimeout(messageId);
538 |         reject(error);
539 |       });
540 |     });
541 |   }
542 | 
543 |   /**
544 |    * Emits a notification, which is a one-way message that does not expect a response.
545 |    */
546 |   async notification(notification: SendNotificationT): Promise<void> {
547 |     if (!this._transport) {
548 |       throw new Error("Not connected");
549 |     }
550 | 
551 |     this.assertNotificationCapability(notification.method);
552 | 
553 |     const jsonrpcNotification: JSONRPCNotification = {
554 |       ...notification,
555 |       jsonrpc: "2.0",
556 |     };
557 | 
558 |     await this._transport.send(jsonrpcNotification);
559 |   }
560 | 
561 |   /**
562 |    * Registers a handler to invoke when this protocol object receives a request with the given method.
563 |    *
564 |    * Note that this will replace any previous request handler for the same method.
565 |    */
566 |   setRequestHandler<
567 |     T extends ZodObject<{
568 |       method: ZodLiteral<string>;
569 |     }>,
570 |   >(
571 |     requestSchema: T,
572 |     handler: (
573 |       request: z.infer<T>,
574 |       extra: RequestHandlerExtra,
575 |     ) => SendResultT | Promise<SendResultT>,
576 |   ): void {
577 |     const method = requestSchema.shape.method.value;
578 |     this.assertRequestHandlerCapability(method);
579 |     this._requestHandlers.set(method, (request, extra) =>
580 |       Promise.resolve(handler(requestSchema.parse(request), extra)),
581 |     );
582 |   }
583 | 
584 |   /**
585 |    * Removes the request handler for the given method.
586 |    */
587 |   removeRequestHandler(method: string): void {
588 |     this._requestHandlers.delete(method);
589 |   }
590 | 
591 |   /**
592 |    * Asserts that a request handler has not already been set for the given method, in preparation for a new one being automatically installed.
593 |    */
594 |   assertCanSetRequestHandler(method: string): void {
595 |     if (this._requestHandlers.has(method)) {
596 |       throw new Error(
597 |         `A request handler for ${method} already exists, which would be overridden`,
598 |       );
599 |     }
600 |   }
601 | 
602 |   /**
603 |    * Registers a handler to invoke when this protocol object receives a notification with the given method.
604 |    *
605 |    * Note that this will replace any previous notification handler for the same method.
606 |    */
607 |   setNotificationHandler<
608 |     T extends ZodObject<{
609 |       method: ZodLiteral<string>;
610 |     }>,
611 |   >(
612 |     notificationSchema: T,
613 |     handler: (notification: z.infer<T>) => void | Promise<void>,
614 |   ): void {
615 |     this._notificationHandlers.set(
616 |       notificationSchema.shape.method.value,
617 |       (notification) =>
618 |         Promise.resolve(handler(notificationSchema.parse(notification))),
619 |     );
620 |   }
621 | 
622 |   /**
623 |    * Removes the notification handler for the given method.
624 |    */
625 |   removeNotificationHandler(method: string): void {
626 |     this._notificationHandlers.delete(method);
627 |   }
628 | }
629 | 
630 | export function mergeCapabilities<
631 |   T extends ServerCapabilities | ClientCapabilities,
632 | >(base: T, additional: T): T {
633 |   return Object.entries(additional).reduce(
634 |     (acc, [key, value]) => {
635 |       if (value && typeof value === "object") {
636 |         acc[key] = acc[key] ? { ...acc[key], ...value } : value;
637 |       } else {
638 |         acc[key] = value;
639 |       }
640 |       return acc;
641 |     },
642 |     { ...base },
643 |   );
644 | }
645 |

 1 | import { JSONRPCMessage } from "../types.js";
 2 | import { ReadBuffer } from "./stdio.js";
 3 | 
 4 | const testMessage: JSONRPCMessage = {
 5 |   jsonrpc: "2.0",
 6 |   method: "foobar",
 7 | };
 8 | 
 9 | test("should have no messages after initialization", () => {
10 |   const readBuffer = new ReadBuffer();
11 |   expect(readBuffer.readMessage()).toBeNull();
12 | });
13 | 
14 | test("should only yield a message after a newline", () => {
15 |   const readBuffer = new ReadBuffer();
16 | 
17 |   readBuffer.append(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(testMessage)));
18 |   expect(readBuffer.readMessage()).toBeNull();
19 | 
20 |   readBuffer.append(Buffer.from("\n"));
21 |   expect(readBuffer.readMessage()).toEqual(testMessage);
22 |   expect(readBuffer.readMessage()).toBeNull();
23 | });
24 | 
25 | test("should be reusable after clearing", () => {
26 |   const readBuffer = new ReadBuffer();
27 | 
28 |   readBuffer.append(Buffer.from("foobar"));
29 |   readBuffer.clear();
30 |   expect(readBuffer.readMessage()).toBeNull();
31 | 
32 |   readBuffer.append(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(testMessage)));
33 |   readBuffer.append(Buffer.from("\n"));
34 |   expect(readBuffer.readMessage()).toEqual(testMessage);
35 | });
36 |

/src/shared/stdio.ts:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 1 | import { JSONRPCMessage, JSONRPCMessageSchema } from "../types.js";
 2 | 
 3 | /**
 4 |  * Buffers a continuous stdio stream into discrete JSON-RPC messages.
 5 |  */
 6 | export class ReadBuffer {
 7 |   private _buffer?: Buffer;
 8 | 
 9 |   append(chunk: Buffer): void {
10 |     this._buffer = this._buffer ? Buffer.concat([this._buffer, chunk]) : chunk;
11 |   }
12 | 
13 |   readMessage(): JSONRPCMessage | null {
14 |     if (!this._buffer) {
15 |       return null;
16 |     }
17 | 
18 |     const index = this._buffer.indexOf("\n");
19 |     if (index === -1) {
20 |       return null;
21 |     }
22 | 
23 |     const line = this._buffer.toString("utf8", 0, index);
24 |     this._buffer = this._buffer.subarray(index + 1);
25 |     return deserializeMessage(line);
26 |   }
27 | 
28 |   clear(): void {
29 |     this._buffer = undefined;
30 |   }
31 | }
32 | 
33 | export function deserializeMessage(line: string): JSONRPCMessage {
34 |   return JSONRPCMessageSchema.parse(JSON.parse(line));
35 | }
36 | 
37 | export function serializeMessage(message: JSONRPCMessage): string {
38 |   return JSON.stringify(message) + "\n";
39 | }
40 |

/src/shared/transport.ts:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 1 | import { JSONRPCMessage } from "../types.js";
 2 | 
 3 | /**
 4 |  * Describes the minimal contract for a MCP transport that a client or server can communicate over.
 5 |  */
 6 | export interface Transport {
 7 |   /**
 8 |    * Starts processing messages on the transport, including any connection steps that might need to be taken.
 9 |    *
10 |    * This method should only be called after callbacks are installed, or else messages may be lost.
11 |    *
12 |    * NOTE: This method should not be called explicitly when using Client, Server, or Protocol classes, as they will implicitly call start().
13 |    */
14 |   start(): Promise<void>;
15 | 
16 |   /**
17 |    * Sends a JSON-RPC message (request or response).
18 |    */
19 |   send(message: JSONRPCMessage): Promise<void>;
20 | 
21 |   /**
22 |    * Closes the connection.
23 |    */
24 |   close(): Promise<void>;
25 | 
26 |   /**
27 |    * Callback for when the connection is closed for any reason.
28 |    *
29 |    * This should be invoked when close() is called as well.
30 |    */
31 |   onclose?: () => void;
32 | 
33 |   /**
34 |    * Callback for when an error occurs.
35 |    *
36 |    * Note that errors are not necessarily fatal; they are used for reporting any kind of exceptional condition out of band.
37 |    */
38 |   onerror?: (error: Error) => void;
39 | 
40 |   /**
41 |    * Callback for when a message (request or response) is received over the connection.
42 |    */
43 |   onmessage?: (message: JSONRPCMessage) => void;
44 | 
45 |   /**
46 |    * The session ID generated for this connection.
47 |    */
48 |   sessionId?: string;
49 | }
50 |
