  1 | import { UriTemplate } from "./uriTemplate.js";
  2 | 
  3 | describe("UriTemplate", () => {
  4 |   describe("isTemplate", () => {
  5 |     it("should return true for strings containing template expressions", () => {
  6 |       expect(UriTemplate.isTemplate("{foo}")).toBe(true);
  7 |       expect(UriTemplate.isTemplate("/users/{id}")).toBe(true);
  8 |       expect(UriTemplate.isTemplate("http://example.com/{path}/{file}")).toBe(true);
  9 |       expect(UriTemplate.isTemplate("/search{?q,limit}")).toBe(true);
 10 |     });
 11 | 
 12 |     it("should return false for strings without template expressions", () => {
 13 |       expect(UriTemplate.isTemplate("")).toBe(false);
 14 |       expect(UriTemplate.isTemplate("plain string")).toBe(false);
 15 |       expect(UriTemplate.isTemplate("http://example.com/foo/bar")).toBe(false);
 16 |       expect(UriTemplate.isTemplate("{}")).toBe(false); // Empty braces don't count
 17 |       expect(UriTemplate.isTemplate("{ }")).toBe(false); // Just whitespace doesn't count
 18 |     });
 19 |   });
 20 | 
 21 |   describe("simple string expansion", () => {
 22 |     it("should expand simple string variables", () => {
 23 |       const template = new UriTemplate("http://example.com/users/{username}");
 24 |       expect(template.expand({ username: "fred" })).toBe(
 25 |         "http://example.com/users/fred",
 26 |       );
 27 |     });
 28 | 
 29 |     it("should handle multiple variables", () => {
 30 |       const template = new UriTemplate("{x,y}");
 31 |       expect(template.expand({ x: "1024", y: "768" })).toBe("1024,768");
 32 |     });
 33 | 
 34 |     it("should encode reserved characters", () => {
 35 |       const template = new UriTemplate("{var}");
 36 |       expect(template.expand({ var: "value with spaces" })).toBe(
 37 |         "value%20with%20spaces",
 38 |       );
 39 |     });
 40 |   });
 41 | 
 42 |   describe("reserved expansion", () => {
 43 |     it("should not encode reserved characters with + operator", () => {
 44 |       const template = new UriTemplate("{+path}/here");
 45 |       expect(template.expand({ path: "/foo/bar" })).toBe("/foo/bar/here");
 46 |     });
 47 |   });
 48 | 
 49 |   describe("fragment expansion", () => {
 50 |     it("should add # prefix and not encode reserved chars", () => {
 51 |       const template = new UriTemplate("X{#var}");
 52 |       expect(template.expand({ var: "/test" })).toBe("X#/test");
 53 |     });
 54 |   });
 55 | 
 56 |   describe("label expansion", () => {
 57 |     it("should add . prefix", () => {
 58 |       const template = new UriTemplate("X{.var}");
 59 |       expect(template.expand({ var: "test" })).toBe("X.test");
 60 |     });
 61 |   });
 62 | 
 63 |   describe("path expansion", () => {
 64 |     it("should add / prefix", () => {
 65 |       const template = new UriTemplate("X{/var}");
 66 |       expect(template.expand({ var: "test" })).toBe("X/test");
 67 |     });
 68 |   });
 69 | 
 70 |   describe("query expansion", () => {
 71 |     it("should add ? prefix and name=value format", () => {
 72 |       const template = new UriTemplate("X{?var}");
 73 |       expect(template.expand({ var: "test" })).toBe("X?var=test");
 74 |     });
 75 |   });
 76 | 
 77 |   describe("form continuation expansion", () => {
 78 |     it("should add & prefix and name=value format", () => {
 79 |       const template = new UriTemplate("X{&var}");
 80 |       expect(template.expand({ var: "test" })).toBe("X&var=test");
 81 |     });
 82 |   });
 83 | 
 84 |   describe("matching", () => {
 85 |     it("should match simple strings and extract variables", () => {
 86 |       const template = new UriTemplate("http://example.com/users/{username}");
 87 |       const match = template.match("http://example.com/users/fred");
 88 |       expect(match).toEqual({ username: "fred" });
 89 |     });
 90 | 
 91 |     it("should match multiple variables", () => {
 92 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/users/{username}/posts/{postId}");
 93 |       const match = template.match("/users/fred/posts/123");
 94 |       expect(match).toEqual({ username: "fred", postId: "123" });
 95 |     });
 96 | 
 97 |     it("should return null for non-matching URIs", () => {
 98 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/users/{username}");
 99 |       const match = template.match("/posts/123");
100 |       expect(match).toBeNull();
101 |     });
102 | 
103 |     it("should handle exploded arrays", () => {
104 |       const template = new UriTemplate("{/list*}");
105 |       const match = template.match("/red,green,blue");
106 |       expect(match).toEqual({ list: ["red", "green", "blue"] });
107 |     });
108 |   });
109 | 
110 |   describe("edge cases", () => {
111 |     it("should handle empty variables", () => {
112 |       const template = new UriTemplate("{empty}");
113 |       expect(template.expand({})).toBe("");
114 |       expect(template.expand({ empty: "" })).toBe("");
115 |     });
116 | 
117 |     it("should handle undefined variables", () => {
118 |       const template = new UriTemplate("{a}{b}{c}");
119 |       expect(template.expand({ b: "2" })).toBe("2");
120 |     });
121 | 
122 |     it("should handle special characters in variable names", () => {
123 |       const template = new UriTemplate("{$var_name}");
124 |       expect(template.expand({ "$var_name": "value" })).toBe("value");
125 |     });
126 |   });
127 | 
128 |   describe("complex patterns", () => {
129 |     it("should handle nested path segments", () => {
130 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/api/{version}/{resource}/{id}");
131 |       expect(template.expand({
132 |         version: "v1",
133 |         resource: "users",
134 |         id: "123"
135 |       })).toBe("/api/v1/users/123");
136 |     });
137 | 
138 |     it("should handle query parameters with arrays", () => {
139 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/search{?tags*}");
140 |       expect(template.expand({
141 |         tags: ["nodejs", "typescript", "testing"]
142 |       })).toBe("/search?tags=nodejs,typescript,testing");
143 |     });
144 | 
145 |     it("should handle multiple query parameters", () => {
146 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/search{?q,page,limit}");
147 |       expect(template.expand({
148 |         q: "test",
149 |         page: "1",
150 |         limit: "10"
151 |       })).toBe("/search?q=test&page=1&limit=10");
152 |     });
153 |   });
154 | 
155 |   describe("matching complex patterns", () => {
156 |     it("should match nested path segments", () => {
157 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/api/{version}/{resource}/{id}");
158 |       const match = template.match("/api/v1/users/123");
159 |       expect(match).toEqual({
160 |         version: "v1",
161 |         resource: "users",
162 |         id: "123"
163 |       });
164 |     });
165 | 
166 |     it("should match query parameters", () => {
167 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/search{?q}");
168 |       const match = template.match("/search?q=test");
169 |       expect(match).toEqual({ q: "test" });
170 |     });
171 | 
172 |     it("should match multiple query parameters", () => {
173 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/search{?q,page}");
174 |       const match = template.match("/search?q=test&page=1");
175 |       expect(match).toEqual({ q: "test", page: "1" });
176 |     });
177 | 
178 |     it("should handle partial matches correctly", () => {
179 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/users/{id}");
180 |       expect(template.match("/users/123/extra")).toBeNull();
181 |       expect(template.match("/users")).toBeNull();
182 |     });
183 |   });
184 | 
185 |   describe("security and edge cases", () => {
186 |     it("should handle extremely long input strings", () => {
187 |       const longString = "x".repeat(100000);
188 |       const template = new UriTemplate(`/api/{param}`);
189 |       expect(template.expand({ param: longString })).toBe(`/api/${longString}`);
190 |       expect(template.match(`/api/${longString}`)).toEqual({ param: longString });
191 |     });
192 | 
193 |     it("should handle deeply nested template expressions", () => {
194 |       const template = new UriTemplate("{a}{b}{c}{d}{e}{f}{g}{h}{i}{j}".repeat(1000));
195 |       expect(() => template.expand({
196 |         a: "1", b: "2", c: "3", d: "4", e: "5",
197 |         f: "6", g: "7", h: "8", i: "9", j: "0"
198 |       })).not.toThrow();
199 |     });
200 | 
201 |     it("should handle malformed template expressions", () => {
202 |       expect(() => new UriTemplate("{unclosed")).toThrow();
203 |       expect(() => new UriTemplate("{}")).not.toThrow();
204 |       expect(() => new UriTemplate("{,}")).not.toThrow();
205 |       expect(() => new UriTemplate("{a}{")).toThrow();
206 |     });
207 | 
208 |     it("should handle pathological regex patterns", () => {
209 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/api/{param}");
210 |       // Create a string that could cause catastrophic backtracking
211 |       const input = "/api/" + "a".repeat(100000);
212 |       expect(() => template.match(input)).not.toThrow();
213 |     });
214 | 
215 |     it("should handle invalid UTF-8 sequences", () => {
216 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/api/{param}");
217 |       const invalidUtf8 = "���";
218 |       expect(() => template.expand({ param: invalidUtf8 })).not.toThrow();
219 |       expect(() => template.match(`/api/${invalidUtf8}`)).not.toThrow();
220 |     });
221 | 
222 |     it("should handle template/URI length mismatches", () => {
223 |       const template = new UriTemplate("/api/{param}");
224 |       expect(template.match("/api/")).toBeNull();
225 |       expect(template.match("/api")).toBeNull();
226 |       expect(template.match("/api/value/extra")).toBeNull();
227 |     });
228 | 
229 |     it("should handle repeated operators", () => {
230 |       const template = new UriTemplate("{?a}{?b}{?c}");
231 |       expect(template.expand({ a: "1", b: "2", c: "3" })).toBe("?a=1&b=2&c=3");
232 |     });
233 | 
234 |     it("should handle overlapping variable names", () => {
235 |       const template = new UriTemplate("{var}{vara}");
236 |       expect(template.expand({ var: "1", vara: "2" })).toBe("12");
237 |     });
238 | 
239 |     it("should handle empty segments", () => {
240 |       const template = new UriTemplate("///{a}////{b}////");
241 |       expect(template.expand({ a: "1", b: "2" })).toBe("///1////2////");
242 |       expect(template.match("///1////2////")).toEqual({ a: "1", b: "2" });
243 |     });
244 | 
245 |     it("should handle maximum template expression limit", () => {
246 |       // Create a template with many expressions
247 |       const expressions = Array(10000).fill("{param}").join("");
248 |       expect(() => new UriTemplate(expressions)).not.toThrow();
249 |     });
250 | 
251 |     it("should handle maximum variable name length", () => {
252 |       const longName = "a".repeat(10000);
253 |       const template = new UriTemplate(`{${longName}}`);
254 |       const vars: Record<string, string> = {};
255 |       vars[longName] = "value";
256 |       expect(() => template.expand(vars)).not.toThrow();
257 |     });
258 |   });
259 | });
260 |

/src/shared/uriTemplate.ts:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1 | // Claude-authored implementation of RFC 6570 URI Templates
  2 | 
  3 | export type Variables = Record<string, string | string[]>;
  4 | 
  5 | const MAX_TEMPLATE_LENGTH = 1000000; // 1MB
  6 | const MAX_VARIABLE_LENGTH = 1000000; // 1MB
  7 | const MAX_TEMPLATE_EXPRESSIONS = 10000;
  8 | const MAX_REGEX_LENGTH = 1000000; // 1MB
  9 | 
 10 | export class UriTemplate {
 11 |   /**
 12 |    * Returns true if the given string contains any URI template expressions.
 13 |    * A template expression is a sequence of characters enclosed in curly braces,
 14 |    * like {foo} or {?bar}.
 15 |    */
 16 |   static isTemplate(str: string): boolean {
 17 |     // Look for any sequence of characters between curly braces
 18 |     // that isn't just whitespace
 19 |     return /\{[^}\s]+\}/.test(str);
 20 |   }
 21 | 
 22 |   private static validateLength(
 23 |     str: string,
 24 |     max: number,
 25 |     context: string,
 26 |   ): void {
 27 |     if (str.length > max) {
 28 |       throw new Error(
 29 |         `${context} exceeds maximum length of ${max} characters (got ${str.length})`,
 30 |       );
 31 |     }
 32 |   }
 33 |   private readonly template: string;
 34 |   private readonly parts: Array<
 35 |     | string
 36 |     | { name: string; operator: string; names: string[]; exploded: boolean }
 37 |   >;
 38 | 
 39 |   constructor(template: string) {
 40 |     UriTemplate.validateLength(template, MAX_TEMPLATE_LENGTH, "Template");
 41 |     this.template = template;
 42 |     this.parts = this.parse(template);
 43 |   }
 44 | 
 45 |   toString(): string {
 46 |     return this.template;
 47 |   }
 48 | 
 49 |   private parse(
 50 |     template: string,
 51 |   ): Array<
 52 |     | string
 53 |     | { name: string; operator: string; names: string[]; exploded: boolean }
 54 |   > {
 55 |     const parts: Array<
 56 |       | string
 57 |       | { name: string; operator: string; names: string[]; exploded: boolean }
 58 |     > = [];
 59 |     let currentText = "";
 60 |     let i = 0;
 61 |     let expressionCount = 0;
 62 | 
 63 |     while (i < template.length) {
 64 |       if (template[i] === "{") {
 65 |         if (currentText) {
 66 |           parts.push(currentText);
 67 |           currentText = "";
 68 |         }
 69 |         const end = template.indexOf("}", i);
 70 |         if (end === -1) throw new Error("Unclosed template expression");
 71 | 
 72 |         expressionCount++;
 73 |         if (expressionCount > MAX_TEMPLATE_EXPRESSIONS) {
 74 |           throw new Error(
 75 |             `Template contains too many expressions (max ${MAX_TEMPLATE_EXPRESSIONS})`,
 76 |           );
 77 |         }
 78 | 
 79 |         const expr = template.slice(i + 1, end);
 80 |         const operator = this.getOperator(expr);
 81 |         const exploded = expr.includes("*");
 82 |         const names = this.getNames(expr);
 83 |         const name = names[0];
 84 | 
 85 |         // Validate variable name length
 86 |         for (const name of names) {
 87 |           UriTemplate.validateLength(
 88 |             name,
 89 |             MAX_VARIABLE_LENGTH,
 90 |             "Variable name",
 91 |           );
 92 |         }
 93 | 
 94 |         parts.push({ name, operator, names, exploded });
 95 |         i = end + 1;
 96 |       } else {
 97 |         currentText += template[i];
 98 |         i++;
 99 |       }
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     if (currentText) {
103 |       parts.push(currentText);
104 |     }
105 | 
106 |     return parts;
107 |   }
108 | 
109 |   private getOperator(expr: string): string {
110 |     const operators = ["+", "#", ".", "/", "?", "&"];
111 |     return operators.find((op) => expr.startsWith(op)) || "";
112 |   }
113 | 
114 |   private getNames(expr: string): string[] {
115 |     const operator = this.getOperator(expr);
116 |     return expr
117 |       .slice(operator.length)
118 |       .split(",")
119 |       .map((name) => name.replace("*", "").trim())
120 |       .filter((name) => name.length > 0);
121 |   }
122 | 
123 |   private encodeValue(value: string, operator: string): string {
124 |     UriTemplate.validateLength(value, MAX_VARIABLE_LENGTH, "Variable value");
125 |     if (operator === "+" || operator === "#") {
126 |       return encodeURI(value);
127 |     }
128 |     return encodeURIComponent(value);
129 |   }
130 | 
131 |   private expandPart(
132 |     part: {
133 |       name: string;
134 |       operator: string;
135 |       names: string[];
136 |       exploded: boolean;
137 |     },
138 |     variables: Variables,
139 |   ): string {
140 |     if (part.operator === "?" || part.operator === "&") {
141 |       const pairs = part.names
142 |         .map((name) => {
143 |           const value = variables[name];
144 |           if (value === undefined) return "";
145 |           const encoded = Array.isArray(value)
146 |             ? value.map((v) => this.encodeValue(v, part.operator)).join(",")
147 |             : this.encodeValue(value.toString(), part.operator);
148 |           return `${name}=${encoded}`;
149 |         })
150 |         .filter((pair) => pair.length > 0);
151 | 
152 |       if (pairs.length === 0) return "";
153 |       const separator = part.operator === "?" ? "?" : "&";
154 |       return separator + pairs.join("&");
155 |     }
156 | 
157 |     if (part.names.length > 1) {
158 |       const values = part.names
159 |         .map((name) => variables[name])
160 |         .filter((v) => v !== undefined);
161 |       if (values.length === 0) return "";
162 |       return values.map((v) => (Array.isArray(v) ? v[0] : v)).join(",");
163 |     }
164 | 
165 |     const value = variables[part.name];
166 |     if (value === undefined) return "";
167 | 
168 |     const values = Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value];
169 |     const encoded = values.map((v) => this.encodeValue(v, part.operator));
170 | 
171 |     switch (part.operator) {
172 |       case "":
173 |         return encoded.join(",");
174 |       case "+":
175 |         return encoded.join(",");
176 |       case "#":
177 |         return "#" + encoded.join(",");
178 |       case ".":
179 |         return "." + encoded.join(".");
180 |       case "/":
181 |         return "/" + encoded.join("/");
182 |       default:
183 |         return encoded.join(",");
184 |     }
185 |   }
186 | 
187 |   expand(variables: Variables): string {
188 |     let result = "";
189 |     let hasQueryParam = false;
190 | 
191 |     for (const part of this.parts) {
192 |       if (typeof part === "string") {
193 |         result += part;
194 |         continue;
195 |       }
196 | 
197 |       const expanded = this.expandPart(part, variables);
198 |       if (!expanded) continue;
199 | 
200 |       // Convert ? to & if we already have a query parameter
201 |       if ((part.operator === "?" || part.operator === "&") && hasQueryParam) {
202 |         result += expanded.replace("?", "&");
203 |       } else {
204 |         result += expanded;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       if (part.operator === "?" || part.operator === "&") {
208 |         hasQueryParam = true;
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 | 
212 |     return result;
213 |   }
214 | 
215 |   private escapeRegExp(str: string): string {
216 |     return str.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\
amp;");
217 |   }
218 | 
219 |   private partToRegExp(part: {
220 |     name: string;
221 |     operator: string;
222 |     names: string[];
223 |     exploded: boolean;
224 |   }): Array<{ pattern: string; name: string }> {
225 |     const patterns: Array<{ pattern: string; name: string }> = [];
226 | 
227 |     // Validate variable name length for matching
228 |     for (const name of part.names) {
229 |       UriTemplate.validateLength(name, MAX_VARIABLE_LENGTH, "Variable name");
230 |     }
231 | 
232 |     if (part.operator === "?" || part.operator === "&") {
233 |       for (let i = 0; i < part.names.length; i++) {
234 |         const name = part.names[i];
235 |         const prefix = i === 0 ? "\\" + part.operator : "&";
236 |         patterns.push({
237 |           pattern: prefix + this.escapeRegExp(name) + "=([^&]+)",
238 |           name,
239 |         });
240 |       }
241 |       return patterns;
242 |     }
243 | 
244 |     let pattern: string;
245 |     const name = part.name;
246 | 
247 |     switch (part.operator) {
248 |       case "":
249 |         pattern = part.exploded ? "([^/]+(?:,[^/]+)*)" : "([^/,]+)";
250 |         break;
251 |       case "+":
252 |       case "#":
253 |         pattern = "(.+)";
254 |         break;
255 |       case ".":
256 |         pattern = "\\.([^/,]+)";
257 |         break;
258 |       case "/":
259 |         pattern = "/" + (part.exploded ? "([^/]+(?:,[^/]+)*)" : "([^/,]+)");
260 |         break;
261 |       default:
262 |         pattern = "([^/]+)";
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     patterns.push({ pattern, name });
266 |     return patterns;
267 |   }
268 | 
269 |   match(uri: string): Variables | null {
270 |     UriTemplate.validateLength(uri, MAX_TEMPLATE_LENGTH, "URI");
271 |     let pattern = "^";
272 |     const names: Array<{ name: string; exploded: boolean }> = [];
273 | 
274 |     for (const part of this.parts) {
275 |       if (typeof part === "string") {
276 |         pattern += this.escapeRegExp(part);
277 |       } else {
278 |         const patterns = this.partToRegExp(part);
279 |         for (const { pattern: partPattern, name } of patterns) {
280 |           pattern += partPattern;
281 |           names.push({ name, exploded: part.exploded });
282 |         }
283 |       }
284 |     }
285 | 
286 |     pattern += "
quot;;
287 |     UriTemplate.validateLength(
288 |       pattern,
289 |       MAX_REGEX_LENGTH,
290 |       "Generated regex pattern",
291 |     );
292 |     const regex = new RegExp(pattern);
293 |     const match = uri.match(regex);
294 | 
295 |     if (!match) return null;
296 | 
297 |     const result: Variables = {};
298 |     for (let i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
299 |       const { name, exploded } = names[i];
300 |       const value = match[i + 1];
301 |       const cleanName = name.replace("*", "");
302 | 
303 |       if (exploded && value.includes(",")) {
304 |         result[cleanName] = value.split(",");
305 |       } else {
306 |         result[cleanName] = value;
307 |       }
308 |     }
309 | 
310 |     return result;
311 |   }
312 | }

   1 | import { z, ZodTypeAny } from "zod";
   2 | 
   3 | export const LATEST_PROTOCOL_VERSION = "2024-11-05";
   4 | export const SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS = [
   5 |   LATEST_PROTOCOL_VERSION,

   9 | /* JSON-RPC types */
  10 | export const JSONRPC_VERSION = "2.0";
  11 | 
  12 | /**
  13 |  * A progress token, used to associate progress notifications with the original request.
  14 |  */
  15 | export const ProgressTokenSchema = z.union([z.string(), z.number().int()]);
  16 | 
  17 | /**
  18 |  * An opaque token used to represent a cursor for pagination.
  19 |  */
  20 | export const CursorSchema = z.string();
  21 | 
  22 | const BaseRequestParamsSchema = z
  23 |   .object({
  24 |     _meta: z.optional(
  25 |       z
  26 |         .object({
  27 |           /**
  28 |            * If specified, the caller is requesting out-of-band progress notifications for this request (as represented by notifications/progress). The value of this parameter is an opaque token that will be attached to any subsequent notifications. The receiver is not obligated to provide these notifications.
  29 |            */
  30 |           progressToken: z.optional(ProgressTokenSchema),
  31 |         })
  32 |         .passthrough(),
  33 |     ),
  34 |   })
  35 |   .passthrough();
  36 | 
  37 | export const RequestSchema = z.object({
  38 |   method: z.string(),
  39 |   params: z.optional(BaseRequestParamsSchema),
  40 | });
  41 | 
  42 | const BaseNotificationParamsSchema = z
  43 |   .object({
  44 |     /**
  45 |      * This parameter name is reserved by MCP to allow clients and servers to attach additional metadata to their notifications.
  46 |      */
  47 |     _meta: z.optional(z.object({}).passthrough()),
  48 |   })
  49 |   .passthrough();
  50 | 
  51 | export const NotificationSchema = z.object({
  52 |   method: z.string(),
  53 |   params: z.optional(BaseNotificationParamsSchema),
  54 | });
  55 | 
  56 | export const ResultSchema = z
  57 |   .object({
  58 |     /**
  59 |      * This result property is reserved by the protocol to allow clients and servers to attach additional metadata to their responses.
  60 |      */
  61 |     _meta: z.optional(z.object({}).passthrough()),
  62 |   })
  63 |   .passthrough();
  64 | 
  65 | /**
  66 |  * A uniquely identifying ID for a request in JSON-RPC.
  67 |  */
  68 | export const RequestIdSchema = z.union([z.string(), z.number().int()]);
  69 | 
  70 | /**
  71 |  * A request that expects a response.
  72 |  */
  73 | export const JSONRPCRequestSchema = z
  74 |   .object({
  75 |     jsonrpc: z.literal(JSONRPC_VERSION),
  76 |     id: RequestIdSchema,
  77 |   })
  78 |   .merge(RequestSchema)
  79 |   .strict();
  80 | 
  81 | /**
  82 |  * A notification which does not expect a response.
  83 |  */
  84 | export const JSONRPCNotificationSchema = z
  85 |   .object({
  86 |     jsonrpc: z.literal(JSONRPC_VERSION),
  87 |   })
  88 |   .merge(NotificationSchema)
  89 |   .strict();
  90 | 
  91 | /**
  92 |  * A successful (non-error) response to a request.
  93 |  */
  94 | export const JSONRPCResponseSchema = z
  95 |   .object({
  96 |     jsonrpc: z.literal(JSONRPC_VERSION),
  97 |     id: RequestIdSchema,
  98 |     result: ResultSchema,
  99 |   })
 100 |   .strict();
 101 | 
 102 | /**
 103 |  * Error codes defined by the JSON-RPC specification.
 104 |  */
 105 | export enum ErrorCode {
 106 |   // SDK error codes
 107 |   ConnectionClosed = -32000,
 108 |   RequestTimeout = -32001,
 109 | 
 110 |   // Standard JSON-RPC error codes
 111 |   ParseError = -32700,
 112 |   InvalidRequest = -32600,
 113 |   MethodNotFound = -32601,
 114 |   InvalidParams = -32602,
 115 |   InternalError = -32603,
 116 | }
 117 | 
 118 | /**
 119 |  * A response to a request that indicates an error occurred.
 120 |  */
 121 | export const JSONRPCErrorSchema = z
 122 |   .object({
 123 |     jsonrpc: z.literal(JSONRPC_VERSION),
 124 |     id: RequestIdSchema,
 125 |     error: z.object({
 126 |       /**
 127 |        * The error type that occurred.
 128 |        */
 129 |       code: z.number().int(),
 130 |       /**
 131 |        * A short description of the error. The message SHOULD be limited to a concise single sentence.
 132 |        */
 133 |       message: z.string(),
 134 |       /**
 135 |        * Additional information about the error. The value of this member is defined by the sender (e.g. detailed error information, nested errors etc.).
 136 |        */
 137 |       data: z.optional(z.unknown()),
 138 |     }),
 139 |   })
 140 |   .strict();
 141 | 
 142 | export const JSONRPCMessageSchema = z.union([
 143 |   JSONRPCRequestSchema,
 144 |   JSONRPCNotificationSchema,
 145 |   JSONRPCResponseSchema,
 146 |   JSONRPCErrorSchema,
 147 | ]);
 148 | 
 149 | /* Empty result */
 150 | /**
 151 |  * A response that indicates success but carries no data.
 152 |  */
 153 | export const EmptyResultSchema = ResultSchema.strict();
 154 | 
 155 | /* Cancellation */
 156 | /**
 157 |  * This notification can be sent by either side to indicate that it is cancelling a previously-issued request.
 158 |  *
 159 |  * The request SHOULD still be in-flight, but due to communication latency, it is always possible that this notification MAY arrive after the request has already finished.
 160 |  *
 161 |  * This notification indicates that the result will be unused, so any associated processing SHOULD cease.
 162 |  *
 163 |  * A client MUST NOT attempt to cancel its `initialize` request.
 164 |  */
 165 | export const CancelledNotificationSchema = NotificationSchema.extend({
 166 |   method: z.literal("notifications/cancelled"),
 167 |   params: BaseNotificationParamsSchema.extend({
 168 |     /**
 169 |      * The ID of the request to cancel.
 170 |      *
 171 |      * This MUST correspond to the ID of a request previously issued in the same direction.
 172 |      */
 173 |     requestId: RequestIdSchema,
 174 | 
 175 |     /**
 176 |      * An optional string describing the reason for the cancellation. This MAY be logged or presented to the user.
 177 |      */
 178 |     reason: z.string().optional(),
 179 |   }),
 180 | });
 181 | 
 182 | /* Initialization */
 183 | /**
 184 |  * Describes the name and version of an MCP implementation.
 185 |  */
 186 | export const ImplementationSchema = z
 187 |   .object({
 188 |     name: z.string(),
 189 |     version: z.string(),
 190 |   })
 191 |   .passthrough();
 192 | 
 193 | /**
 194 |  * Capabilities a client may support. Known capabilities are defined here, in this schema, but this is not a closed set: any client can define its own, additional capabilities.
 195 |  */
 196 | export const ClientCapabilitiesSchema = z
 197 |   .object({
 198 |     /**
 199 |      * Experimental, non-standard capabilities that the client supports.
 200 |      */
 201 |     experimental: z.optional(z.object({}).passthrough()),
 202 |     /**
 203 |      * Present if the client supports sampling from an LLM.
 204 |      */
 205 |     sampling: z.optional(z.object({}).passthrough()),
 206 |     /**
 207 |      * Present if the client supports listing roots.
 208 |      */
 209 |     roots: z.optional(
 210 |       z
 211 |         .object({
 212 |           /**
 213 |            * Whether the client supports issuing notifications for changes to the roots list.
 214 |            */
 215 |           listChanged: z.optional(z.boolean()),
 216 |         })
 217 |         .passthrough(),
 218 |     ),
 219 |   })
 220 |   .passthrough();
 221 | 
 222 | /**
 223 |  * This request is sent from the client to the server when it first connects, asking it to begin initialization.
 224 |  */
 225 | export const InitializeRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 226 |   method: z.literal("initialize"),
 227 |   params: BaseRequestParamsSchema.extend({
 228 |     /**
 229 |      * The latest version of the Model Context Protocol that the client supports. The client MAY decide to support older versions as well.
 230 |      */
 231 |     protocolVersion: z.string(),
 232 |     capabilities: ClientCapabilitiesSchema,
 233 |     clientInfo: ImplementationSchema,
 234 |   }),
 235 | });
 236 | 
 237 | /**
 238 |  * Capabilities that a server may support. Known capabilities are defined here, in this schema, but this is not a closed set: any server can define its own, additional capabilities.
 239 |  */
 240 | export const ServerCapabilitiesSchema = z
 241 |   .object({
 242 |     /**
 243 |      * Experimental, non-standard capabilities that the server supports.
 244 |      */
 245 |     experimental: z.optional(z.object({}).passthrough()),
 246 |     /**
 247 |      * Present if the server supports sending log messages to the client.
 248 |      */
 249 |     logging: z.optional(z.object({}).passthrough()),
 250 |     /**
 251 |      * Present if the server offers any prompt templates.
 252 |      */
 253 |     prompts: z.optional(
 254 |       z
 255 |         .object({
 256 |           /**
 257 |            * Whether this server supports issuing notifications for changes to the prompt list.
 258 |            */
 259 |           listChanged: z.optional(z.boolean()),
 260 |         })
 261 |         .passthrough(),
 262 |     ),
 263 |     /**
 264 |      * Present if the server offers any resources to read.
 265 |      */
 266 |     resources: z.optional(
 267 |       z
 268 |         .object({
 269 |           /**
 270 |            * Whether this server supports clients subscribing to resource updates.
 271 |            */
 272 |           subscribe: z.optional(z.boolean()),
 273 | 
 274 |           /**
 275 |            * Whether this server supports issuing notifications for changes to the resource list.
 276 |            */
 277 |           listChanged: z.optional(z.boolean()),
 278 |         })
 279 |         .passthrough(),
 280 |     ),
 281 |     /**
 282 |      * Present if the server offers any tools to call.
 283 |      */
 284 |     tools: z.optional(
 285 |       z
 286 |         .object({
 287 |           /**
 288 |            * Whether this server supports issuing notifications for changes to the tool list.
 289 |            */
 290 |           listChanged: z.optional(z.boolean()),
 291 |         })
 292 |         .passthrough(),
 293 |     ),
 294 |   })
 295 |   .passthrough();

 297 | /**
 298 |  * After receiving an initialize request from the client, the server sends this response.
 299 |  */
 300 | export const InitializeResultSchema = ResultSchema.extend({
 301 |   /**
 302 |    * The version of the Model Context Protocol that the server wants to use. This may not match the version that the client requested. If the client cannot support this version, it MUST disconnect.
 303 |    */
 304 |   protocolVersion: z.string(),
 305 |   capabilities: ServerCapabilitiesSchema,
 306 |   serverInfo: ImplementationSchema,
 307 |   /**
 308 |    * Instructions describing how to use the server and its features.
 309 |    *
 310 |    * This can be used by clients to improve the LLM's understanding of available tools, resources, etc. It can be thought of like a "hint" to the model. For example, this information MAY be added to the system prompt.
 311 |    */
 312 |   instructions: z.optional(z.string()),
 313 | });
 314 | 
 315 | /**
 316 |  * This notification is sent from the client to the server after initialization has finished.
 317 |  */
 318 | export const InitializedNotificationSchema = NotificationSchema.extend({
 319 |   method: z.literal("notifications/initialized"),
 320 | });
 321 | 
 322 | /* Ping */
 323 | /**
 324 |  * A ping, issued by either the server or the client, to check that the other party is still alive. The receiver must promptly respond, or else may be disconnected.
 325 |  */
 326 | export const PingRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 327 |   method: z.literal("ping"),
 328 | });
 329 | 
 330 | /* Progress notifications */
 331 | export const ProgressSchema = z
 332 |   .object({
 333 |     /**
 334 |      * The progress thus far. This should increase every time progress is made, even if the total is unknown.
 335 |      */
 336 |     progress: z.number(),
 337 |     /**
 338 |      * Total number of items to process (or total progress required), if known.
 339 |      */
 340 |     total: z.optional(z.number()),
 341 |   })
 342 |   .passthrough();
 343 | 
 344 | /**
 345 |  * An out-of-band notification used to inform the receiver of a progress update for a long-running request.
 346 |  */
 347 | export const ProgressNotificationSchema = NotificationSchema.extend({
 348 |   method: z.literal("notifications/progress"),
 349 |   params: BaseNotificationParamsSchema.merge(ProgressSchema).extend({
 350 |     /**
 351 |      * The progress token which was given in the initial request, used to associate this notification with the request that is proceeding.
 352 |      */
 353 |     progressToken: ProgressTokenSchema,
 354 |   }),
 355 | });
 356 | 
 357 | /* Pagination */
 358 | export const PaginatedRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 359 |   params: BaseRequestParamsSchema.extend({
 360 |     /**
 361 |      * An opaque token representing the current pagination position.
 362 |      * If provided, the server should return results starting after this cursor.
 363 |      */
 364 |     cursor: z.optional(CursorSchema),
 365 |   }).optional(),
 366 | });
 367 | 
 368 | export const PaginatedResultSchema = ResultSchema.extend({
 369 |   /**
 370 |    * An opaque token representing the pagination position after the last returned result.
 371 |    * If present, there may be more results available.
 372 |    */
 373 |   nextCursor: z.optional(CursorSchema),
 374 | });
 375 | 
 376 | /* Resources */
 377 | /**
 378 |  * The contents of a specific resource or sub-resource.
 379 |  */
 380 | export const ResourceContentsSchema = z
 381 |   .object({
 382 |     /**
 383 |      * The URI of this resource.
 384 |      */
 385 |     uri: z.string(),
 386 |     /**
 387 |      * The MIME type of this resource, if known.
 388 |      */
 389 |     mimeType: z.optional(z.string()),
 390 |   })
 391 |   .passthrough();
 392 | 
 393 | export const TextResourceContentsSchema = ResourceContentsSchema.extend({
 394 |   /**
 395 |    * The text of the item. This must only be set if the item can actually be represented as text (not binary data).
 396 |    */
 397 |   text: z.string(),
 398 | });
 399 | 
 400 | export const BlobResourceContentsSchema = ResourceContentsSchema.extend({
 401 |   /**
 402 |    * A base64-encoded string representing the binary data of the item.
 403 |    */
 404 |   blob: z.string().base64(),
 405 | });
 406 | 
 407 | /**
 408 |  * A known resource that the server is capable of reading.
 409 |  */
 410 | export const ResourceSchema = z
 411 |   .object({
 412 |     /**
 413 |      * The URI of this resource.
 414 |      */
 415 |     uri: z.string(),
 416 | 
 417 |     /**
 418 |      * A human-readable name for this resource.
 419 |      *
 420 |      * This can be used by clients to populate UI elements.
 421 |      */
 422 |     name: z.string(),
 423 | 
 424 |     /**
 425 |      * A description of what this resource represents.
 426 |      *
 427 |      * This can be used by clients to improve the LLM's understanding of available resources. It can be thought of like a "hint" to the model.
 428 |      */
 429 |     description: z.optional(z.string()),
 430 | 
 431 |     /**
 432 |      * The MIME type of this resource, if known.
 433 |      */
 434 |     mimeType: z.optional(z.string()),
 435 |   })
 436 |   .passthrough();
 437 | 
 438 | /**
 439 |  * A template description for resources available on the server.
 440 |  */
 441 | export const ResourceTemplateSchema = z
 442 |   .object({
 443 |     /**
 444 |      * A URI template (according to RFC 6570) that can be used to construct resource URIs.
 445 |      */
 446 |     uriTemplate: z.string(),
 447 | 
 448 |     /**
 449 |      * A human-readable name for the type of resource this template refers to.
 450 |      *
 451 |      * This can be used by clients to populate UI elements.
 452 |      */
 453 |     name: z.string(),
 454 | 
 455 |     /**
 456 |      * A description of what this template is for.
 457 |      *
 458 |      * This can be used by clients to improve the LLM's understanding of available resources. It can be thought of like a "hint" to the model.
 459 |      */
 460 |     description: z.optional(z.string()),
 461 | 
 462 |     /**
 463 |      * The MIME type for all resources that match this template. This should only be included if all resources matching this template have the same type.
 464 |      */
 465 |     mimeType: z.optional(z.string()),
 466 |   })
 467 |   .passthrough();
 468 | 
 469 | /**
 470 |  * Sent from the client to request a list of resources the server has.
 471 |  */
 472 | export const ListResourcesRequestSchema = PaginatedRequestSchema.extend({
 473 |   method: z.literal("resources/list"),
 474 | });
 475 | 
 476 | /**
 477 |  * The server's response to a resources/list request from the client.
 478 |  */
 479 | export const ListResourcesResultSchema = PaginatedResultSchema.extend({
 480 |   resources: z.array(ResourceSchema),
 481 | });
 482 | 
 483 | /**
 484 |  * Sent from the client to request a list of resource templates the server has.
 485 |  */
 486 | export const ListResourceTemplatesRequestSchema = PaginatedRequestSchema.extend(
 487 |   {
 488 |     method: z.literal("resources/templates/list"),
 489 |   },
 490 | );
 491 | 
 492 | /**
 493 |  * The server's response to a resources/templates/list request from the client.
 494 |  */
 495 | export const ListResourceTemplatesResultSchema = PaginatedResultSchema.extend({
 496 |   resourceTemplates: z.array(ResourceTemplateSchema),
 497 | });
 498 | 
 499 | /**
 500 |  * Sent from the client to the server, to read a specific resource URI.
 501 |  */
 502 | export const ReadResourceRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 503 |   method: z.literal("resources/read"),
 504 |   params: BaseRequestParamsSchema.extend({
 505 |     /**
 506 |      * The URI of the resource to read. The URI can use any protocol; it is up to the server how to interpret it.
 507 |      */
 508 |     uri: z.string(),
 509 |   }),
 510 | });
 511 | 
 512 | /**
 513 |  * The server's response to a resources/read request from the client.
 514 |  */
 515 | export const ReadResourceResultSchema = ResultSchema.extend({
 516 |   contents: z.array(
 517 |     z.union([TextResourceContentsSchema, BlobResourceContentsSchema]),
 518 |   ),
 519 | });
 520 | 
 521 | /**
 522 |  * An optional notification from the server to the client, informing it that the list of resources it can read from has changed. This may be issued by servers without any previous subscription from the client.
 523 |  */
 524 | export const ResourceListChangedNotificationSchema = NotificationSchema.extend({
 525 |   method: z.literal("notifications/resources/list_changed"),
 526 | });
 527 | 
 528 | /**
 529 |  * Sent from the client to request resources/updated notifications from the server whenever a particular resource changes.
 530 |  */
 531 | export const SubscribeRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 532 |   method: z.literal("resources/subscribe"),
 533 |   params: BaseRequestParamsSchema.extend({
 534 |     /**
 535 |      * The URI of the resource to subscribe to. The URI can use any protocol; it is up to the server how to interpret it.
 536 |      */
 537 |     uri: z.string(),
 538 |   }),
 539 | });
 540 | 
 541 | /**
 542 |  * Sent from the client to request cancellation of resources/updated notifications from the server. This should follow a previous resources/subscribe request.
 543 |  */
 544 | export const UnsubscribeRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 545 |   method: z.literal("resources/unsubscribe"),
 546 |   params: BaseRequestParamsSchema.extend({
 547 |     /**
 548 |      * The URI of the resource to unsubscribe from.
 549 |      */
 550 |     uri: z.string(),
 551 |   }),
 552 | });
 553 | 
 554 | /**
 555 |  * A notification from the server to the client, informing it that a resource has changed and may need to be read again. This should only be sent if the client previously sent a resources/subscribe request.
 556 |  */
 557 | export const ResourceUpdatedNotificationSchema = NotificationSchema.extend({
 558 |   method: z.literal("notifications/resources/updated"),
 559 |   params: BaseNotificationParamsSchema.extend({
 560 |     /**
 561 |      * The URI of the resource that has been updated. This might be a sub-resource of the one that the client actually subscribed to.
 562 |      */
 563 |     uri: z.string(),
 564 |   }),
 565 | });
 566 | 
 567 | /* Prompts */
 568 | /**
 569 |  * Describes an argument that a prompt can accept.
 570 |  */
 571 | export const PromptArgumentSchema = z
 572 |   .object({
 573 |     /**
 574 |      * The name of the argument.
 575 |      */
 576 |     name: z.string(),
 577 |     /**
 578 |      * A human-readable description of the argument.
 579 |      */
 580 |     description: z.optional(z.string()),
 581 |     /**
 582 |      * Whether this argument must be provided.
 583 |      */
 584 |     required: z.optional(z.boolean()),
 585 |   })
 586 |   .passthrough();
 587 | 
 588 | /**
 589 |  * A prompt or prompt template that the server offers.
 590 |  */
 591 | export const PromptSchema = z
 592 |   .object({
 593 |     /**
 594 |      * The name of the prompt or prompt template.
 595 |      */
 596 |     name: z.string(),
 597 |     /**
 598 |      * An optional description of what this prompt provides
 599 |      */
 600 |     description: z.optional(z.string()),
 601 |     /**
 602 |      * A list of arguments to use for templating the prompt.
 603 |      */
 604 |     arguments: z.optional(z.array(PromptArgumentSchema)),
 605 |   })
 606 |   .passthrough();
 607 | 
 608 | /**
 609 |  * Sent from the client to request a list of prompts and prompt templates the server has.
 610 |  */
 611 | export const ListPromptsRequestSchema = PaginatedRequestSchema.extend({
 612 |   method: z.literal("prompts/list"),
 613 | });
 614 | 
 615 | /**
 616 |  * The server's response to a prompts/list request from the client.
 617 |  */
 618 | export const ListPromptsResultSchema = PaginatedResultSchema.extend({
 619 |   prompts: z.array(PromptSchema),
 620 | });
 621 | 
 622 | /**
 623 |  * Used by the client to get a prompt provided by the server.
 624 |  */
 625 | export const GetPromptRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 626 |   method: z.literal("prompts/get"),
 627 |   params: BaseRequestParamsSchema.extend({
 628 |     /**
 629 |      * The name of the prompt or prompt template.
 630 |      */
 631 |     name: z.string(),
 632 |     /**
 633 |      * Arguments to use for templating the prompt.
 634 |      */
 635 |     arguments: z.optional(z.record(z.string())),
 636 |   }),
 637 | });
 638 | 
 639 | /**
 640 |  * Text provided to or from an LLM.
 641 |  */
 642 | export const TextContentSchema = z
 643 |   .object({
 644 |     type: z.literal("text"),
 645 |     /**
 646 |      * The text content of the message.
 647 |      */
 648 |     text: z.string(),
 649 |   })
 650 |   .passthrough();
 651 | 
 652 | /**
 653 |  * An image provided to or from an LLM.
 654 |  */
 655 | export const ImageContentSchema = z
 656 |   .object({
 657 |     type: z.literal("image"),
 658 |     /**
 659 |      * The base64-encoded image data.
 660 |      */
 661 |     data: z.string().base64(),
 662 |     /**
 663 |      * The MIME type of the image. Different providers may support different image types.
 664 |      */
 665 |     mimeType: z.string(),
 666 |   })
 667 |   .passthrough();
 668 | 
 669 | /**
 670 |  * The contents of a resource, embedded into a prompt or tool call result.
 671 |  */
 672 | export const EmbeddedResourceSchema = z
 673 |   .object({
 674 |     type: z.literal("resource"),
 675 |     resource: z.union([TextResourceContentsSchema, BlobResourceContentsSchema]),
 676 |   })
 677 |   .passthrough();
 678 | 
 679 | /**
 680 |  * Describes a message returned as part of a prompt.
 681 |  */
 682 | export const PromptMessageSchema = z
 683 |   .object({
 684 |     role: z.enum(["user", "assistant"]),
 685 |     content: z.union([
 686 |       TextContentSchema,
 687 |       ImageContentSchema,
 688 |       EmbeddedResourceSchema,
 689 |     ]),
 690 |   })
 691 |   .passthrough();
 692 | 
 693 | /**
 694 |  * The server's response to a prompts/get request from the client.
 695 |  */
 696 | export const GetPromptResultSchema = ResultSchema.extend({
 697 |   /**
 698 |    * An optional description for the prompt.
 699 |    */
 700 |   description: z.optional(z.string()),
 701 |   messages: z.array(PromptMessageSchema),
 702 | });
 703 | 
 704 | /**
 705 |  * An optional notification from the server to the client, informing it that the list of prompts it offers has changed. This may be issued by servers without any previous subscription from the client.
 706 |  */
 707 | export const PromptListChangedNotificationSchema = NotificationSchema.extend({
 708 |   method: z.literal("notifications/prompts/list_changed"),
 709 | });
 710 | 
 711 | /* Tools */
 712 | /**
 713 |  * Definition for a tool the client can call.
 714 |  */
 715 | export const ToolSchema = z
 716 |   .object({
 717 |     /**
 718 |      * The name of the tool.
 719 |      */
 720 |     name: z.string(),
 721 |     /**
 722 |      * A human-readable description of the tool.
 723 |      */
 724 |     description: z.optional(z.string()),
 725 |     /**
 726 |      * A JSON Schema object defining the expected parameters for the tool.
 727 |      */
 728 |     inputSchema: z
 729 |       .object({
 730 |         type: z.literal("object"),
 731 |         properties: z.optional(z.object({}).passthrough()),
 732 |       })
 733 |       .passthrough(),
 734 |   })
 735 |   .passthrough();
 736 | 
 737 | /**
 738 |  * Sent from the client to request a list of tools the server has.
 739 |  */
 740 | export const ListToolsRequestSchema = PaginatedRequestSchema.extend({
 741 |   method: z.literal("tools/list"),
 742 | });
 743 | 
 744 | /**
 745 |  * The server's response to a tools/list request from the client.
 746 |  */
 747 | export const ListToolsResultSchema = PaginatedResultSchema.extend({
 748 |   tools: z.array(ToolSchema),
 749 | });
 750 | 
 751 | /**
 752 |  * The server's response to a tool call.
 753 |  */
 754 | export const CallToolResultSchema = ResultSchema.extend({
 755 |   content: z.array(
 756 |     z.union([TextContentSchema, ImageContentSchema, EmbeddedResourceSchema]),
 757 |   ),
 758 |   isError: z.boolean().default(false).optional(),
 759 | });
 760 | 
 761 | /**
 762 |  * CallToolResultSchema extended with backwards compatibility to protocol version 2024-10-07.
 763 |  */
 764 | export const CompatibilityCallToolResultSchema = CallToolResultSchema.or(
 765 |   ResultSchema.extend({
 766 |     toolResult: z.unknown(),
 767 |   }),
 768 | );
 769 | 
 770 | /**
 771 |  * Used by the client to invoke a tool provided by the server.
 772 |  */
 773 | export const CallToolRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 774 |   method: z.literal("tools/call"),
 775 |   params: BaseRequestParamsSchema.extend({
 776 |     name: z.string(),
 777 |     arguments: z.optional(z.record(z.unknown())),
 778 |   }),
 779 | });
 780 | 
 781 | /**
 782 |  * An optional notification from the server to the client, informing it that the list of tools it offers has changed. This may be issued by servers without any previous subscription from the client.
 783 |  */
 784 | export const ToolListChangedNotificationSchema = NotificationSchema.extend({
 785 |   method: z.literal("notifications/tools/list_changed"),
 786 | });
 787 | 
 788 | /* Logging */
 789 | /**
 790 |  * The severity of a log message.
 791 |  */
 792 | export const LoggingLevelSchema = z.enum([
 793 |   "debug",
 794 |   "info",
 795 |   "notice",
 796 |   "warning",
 797 |   "error",
 798 |   "critical",
 799 |   "alert",
 800 |   "emergency",
 801 | ]);
 802 | 
 803 | /**
 804 |  * A request from the client to the server, to enable or adjust logging.
 805 |  */
 806 | export const SetLevelRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 807 |   method: z.literal("logging/setLevel"),
 808 |   params: BaseRequestParamsSchema.extend({
 809 |     /**
 810 |      * The level of logging that the client wants to receive from the server. The server should send all logs at this level and higher (i.e., more severe) to the client as notifications/logging/message.
 811 |      */
 812 |     level: LoggingLevelSchema,
 813 |   }),
 814 | });
 815 | 
 816 | /**
 817 |  * Notification of a log message passed from server to client. If no logging/setLevel request has been sent from the client, the server MAY decide which messages to send automatically.
 818 |  */
 819 | export const LoggingMessageNotificationSchema = NotificationSchema.extend({
 820 |   method: z.literal("notifications/message"),
 821 |   params: BaseNotificationParamsSchema.extend({
 822 |     /**
 823 |      * The severity of this log message.
 824 |      */
 825 |     level: LoggingLevelSchema,
 826 |     /**
 827 |      * An optional name of the logger issuing this message.
 828 |      */
 829 |     logger: z.optional(z.string()),
 830 |     /**
 831 |      * The data to be logged, such as a string message or an object. Any JSON serializable type is allowed here.
 832 |      */
 833 |     data: z.unknown(),
 834 |   }),
 835 | });
 836 | 
 837 | /* Sampling */
 838 | /**
 839 |  * Hints to use for model selection.
 840 |  */
 841 | export const ModelHintSchema = z
 842 |   .object({
 843 |     /**
 844 |      * A hint for a model name.
 845 |      */
 846 |     name: z.string().optional(),
 847 |   })
 848 |   .passthrough();
 849 | 
 850 | /**
 851 |  * The server's preferences for model selection, requested of the client during sampling.
 852 |  */
 853 | export const ModelPreferencesSchema = z
 854 |   .object({
 855 |     /**
 856 |      * Optional hints to use for model selection.
 857 |      */
 858 |     hints: z.optional(z.array(ModelHintSchema)),
 859 |     /**
 860 |      * How much to prioritize cost when selecting a model.
 861 |      */
 862 |     costPriority: z.optional(z.number().min(0).max(1)),
 863 |     /**
 864 |      * How much to prioritize sampling speed (latency) when selecting a model.
 865 |      */
 866 |     speedPriority: z.optional(z.number().min(0).max(1)),
 867 |     /**
 868 |      * How much to prioritize intelligence and capabilities when selecting a model.
 869 |      */
 870 |     intelligencePriority: z.optional(z.number().min(0).max(1)),
 871 |   })
 872 |   .passthrough();
 873 | 
 874 | /**
 875 |  * Describes a message issued to or received from an LLM API.
 876 |  */
 877 | export const SamplingMessageSchema = z
 878 |   .object({
 879 |     role: z.enum(["user", "assistant"]),
 880 |     content: z.union([TextContentSchema, ImageContentSchema]),
 881 |   })
 882 |   .passthrough();
 883 | 
 884 | /**
 885 |  * A request from the server to sample an LLM via the client. The client has full discretion over which model to select. The client should also inform the user before beginning sampling, to allow them to inspect the request (human in the loop) and decide whether to approve it.
 886 |  */
 887 | export const CreateMessageRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 888 |   method: z.literal("sampling/createMessage"),
 889 |   params: BaseRequestParamsSchema.extend({
 890 |     messages: z.array(SamplingMessageSchema),
 891 |     /**
 892 |      * An optional system prompt the server wants to use for sampling. The client MAY modify or omit this prompt.
 893 |      */
 894 |     systemPrompt: z.optional(z.string()),
 895 |     /**
 896 |      * A request to include context from one or more MCP servers (including the caller), to be attached to the prompt. The client MAY ignore this request.
 897 |      */
 898 |     includeContext: z.optional(z.enum(["none", "thisServer", "allServers"])),
 899 |     temperature: z.optional(z.number()),
 900 |     /**
 901 |      * The maximum number of tokens to sample, as requested by the server. The client MAY choose to sample fewer tokens than requested.
 902 |      */
 903 |     maxTokens: z.number().int(),
 904 |     stopSequences: z.optional(z.array(z.string())),
 905 |     /**
 906 |      * Optional metadata to pass through to the LLM provider. The format of this metadata is provider-specific.
 907 |      */
 908 |     metadata: z.optional(z.object({}).passthrough()),
 909 |     /**
 910 |      * The server's preferences for which model to select.
 911 |      */
 912 |     modelPreferences: z.optional(ModelPreferencesSchema),
 913 |   }),
 914 | });
 915 | 
 916 | /**
 917 |  * The client's response to a sampling/create_message request from the server. The client should inform the user before returning the sampled message, to allow them to inspect the response (human in the loop) and decide whether to allow the server to see it.
 918 |  */
 919 | export const CreateMessageResultSchema = ResultSchema.extend({
 920 |   /**
 921 |    * The name of the model that generated the message.
 922 |    */
 923 |   model: z.string(),
 924 |   /**
 925 |    * The reason why sampling stopped.
 926 |    */
 927 |   stopReason: z.optional(
 928 |     z.enum(["endTurn", "stopSequence", "maxTokens"]).or(z.string()),
 929 |   ),
 930 |   role: z.enum(["user", "assistant"]),
 931 |   content: z.discriminatedUnion("type", [
 932 |     TextContentSchema,
 933 |     ImageContentSchema,
 934 |   ]),
 935 | });
 936 | 
 937 | /* Autocomplete */
 938 | /**
 939 |  * A reference to a resource or resource template definition.
 940 |  */
 941 | export const ResourceReferenceSchema = z
 942 |   .object({
 943 |     type: z.literal("ref/resource"),
 944 |     /**
 945 |      * The URI or URI template of the resource.
 946 |      */
 947 |     uri: z.string(),
 948 |   })
 949 |   .passthrough();
 950 | 
 951 | /**
 952 |  * Identifies a prompt.
 953 |  */
 954 | export const PromptReferenceSchema = z
 955 |   .object({
 956 |     type: z.literal("ref/prompt"),
 957 |     /**
 958 |      * The name of the prompt or prompt template
 959 |      */
 960 |     name: z.string(),
 961 |   })
 962 |   .passthrough();
 963 | 
 964 | /**
 965 |  * A request from the client to the server, to ask for completion options.
 966 |  */
 967 | export const CompleteRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
 968 |   method: z.literal("completion/complete"),
 969 |   params: BaseRequestParamsSchema.extend({
 970 |     ref: z.union([PromptReferenceSchema, ResourceReferenceSchema]),
 971 |     /**
 972 |      * The argument's information
 973 |      */
 974 |     argument: z
 975 |       .object({
 976 |         /**
 977 |          * The name of the argument
 978 |          */
 979 |         name: z.string(),
 980 |         /**
 981 |          * The value of the argument to use for completion matching.
 982 |          */
 983 |         value: z.string(),
 984 |       })
 985 |       .passthrough(),
 986 |   }),
 987 | });
 988 | 
 989 | /**
 990 |  * The server's response to a completion/complete request
 991 |  */
 992 | export const CompleteResultSchema = ResultSchema.extend({
 993 |   completion: z
 994 |     .object({
 995 |       /**
 996 |        * An array of completion values. Must not exceed 100 items.
 997 |        */
 998 |       values: z.array(z.string()).max(100),
 999 |       /**
1000 |        * The total number of completion options available. This can exceed the number of values actually sent in the response.
1001 |        */
1002 |       total: z.optional(z.number().int()),
1003 |       /**
1004 |        * Indicates whether there are additional completion options beyond those provided in the current response, even if the exact total is unknown.
1005 |        */
1006 |       hasMore: z.optional(z.boolean()),
1007 |     })
1008 |     .passthrough(),
1009 | });
1010 | 
1011 | /* Roots */
1012 | /**
1013 |  * Represents a root directory or file that the server can operate on.
1014 |  */
1015 | export const RootSchema = z
1016 |   .object({
1017 |     /**
1018 |      * The URI identifying the root. This *must* start with file:// for now.
1019 |      */
1020 |     uri: z.string().startsWith("file://"),
1021 |     /**
1022 |      * An optional name for the root.
1023 |      */
1024 |     name: z.optional(z.string()),
1025 |   })
1026 |   .passthrough();
1027 | 
1028 | /**
1029 |  * Sent from the server to request a list of root URIs from the client.
1030 |  */
1031 | export const ListRootsRequestSchema = RequestSchema.extend({
1032 |   method: z.literal("roots/list"),
1033 | });
1034 | 
1035 | /**
1036 |  * The client's response to a roots/list request from the server.
1037 |  */
1038 | export const ListRootsResultSchema = ResultSchema.extend({
1039 |   roots: z.array(RootSchema),
1040 | });
1041 | 
1042 | /**
1043 |  * A notification from the client to the server, informing it that the list of roots has changed.
1044 |  */
1045 | export const RootsListChangedNotificationSchema = NotificationSchema.extend({
1046 |   method: z.literal("notifications/roots/list_changed"),
1047 | });
1048 | 
1049 | /* Client messages */
1050 | export const ClientRequestSchema = z.union([
1051 |   PingRequestSchema,
1052 |   InitializeRequestSchema,
1053 |   CompleteRequestSchema,
1054 |   SetLevelRequestSchema,
1055 |   GetPromptRequestSchema,
1056 |   ListPromptsRequestSchema,
1057 |   ListResourcesRequestSchema,
1058 |   ListResourceTemplatesRequestSchema,
1059 |   ReadResourceRequestSchema,
1060 |   SubscribeRequestSchema,
1061 |   UnsubscribeRequestSchema,
1062 |   CallToolRequestSchema,
1063 |   ListToolsRequestSchema,
1064 | ]);
1065 | 
1066 | export const ClientNotificationSchema = z.union([
1067 |   CancelledNotificationSchema,
1068 |   ProgressNotificationSchema,
1069 |   InitializedNotificationSchema,
1070 |   RootsListChangedNotificationSchema,
1071 | ]);
1072 | 
1073 | export const ClientResultSchema = z.union([
1074 |   EmptyResultSchema,
1075 |   CreateMessageResultSchema,
1076 |   ListRootsResultSchema,
1077 | ]);
1078 | 
1079 | /* Server messages */
1080 | export const ServerRequestSchema = z.union([
1081 |   PingRequestSchema,
1082 |   CreateMessageRequestSchema,
1083 |   ListRootsRequestSchema,
1084 | ]);
1085 | 
1086 | export const ServerNotificationSchema = z.union([
1087 |   CancelledNotificationSchema,
1088 |   ProgressNotificationSchema,
1089 |   LoggingMessageNotificationSchema,
1090 |   ResourceUpdatedNotificationSchema,
1091 |   ResourceListChangedNotificationSchema,
1092 |   ToolListChangedNotificationSchema,
1093 |   PromptListChangedNotificationSchema,
1094 | ]);
1095 | 
1096 | export const ServerResultSchema = z.union([
1097 |   EmptyResultSchema,
1098 |   InitializeResultSchema,
1099 |   CompleteResultSchema,
1100 |   GetPromptResultSchema,
1101 |   ListPromptsResultSchema,
1102 |   ListResourcesResultSchema,
1103 |   ListResourceTemplatesResultSchema,
1104 |   ReadResourceResultSchema,
1105 |   CallToolResultSchema,
1106 |   ListToolsResultSchema,
1107 | ]);
1108 | 
1109 | export class McpError extends Error {
1110 |   constructor(
1111 |     public readonly code: number,
1112 |     message: string,
1113 |     public readonly data?: unknown,
1114 |   ) {
1115 |     super(`MCP error ${code}: ${message}`);
1116 |     this.name = "McpError";
1117 |   }
1118 | }
1119 | 
1120 | type Primitive = string | number | boolean | bigint | null | undefined;
1121 | type Flatten<T> = T extends Primitive
1122 |   ? T
1123 |   : T extends Array<infer U>
1124 |   ? Array<Flatten<U>>
1125 |   : T extends Set<infer U>
1126 |   ? Set<Flatten<U>>
1127 |   : T extends Map<infer K, infer V>
1128 |   ? Map<Flatten<K>, Flatten<V>>
1129 |   : T extends object
1130 |   ? { [K in keyof T]: Flatten<T[K]> }
1131 |   : T;
1132 | 
1133 | type Infer<Schema extends ZodTypeAny> = Flatten<z.infer<Schema>>;
1134 | 
1135 | /* JSON-RPC types */
1136 | export type ProgressToken = Infer<typeof ProgressTokenSchema>;
1137 | export type Cursor = Infer<typeof CursorSchema>;
1138 | export type Request = Infer<typeof RequestSchema>;
1139 | export type Notification = Infer<typeof NotificationSchema>;
1140 | export type Result = Infer<typeof ResultSchema>;
1141 | export type RequestId = Infer<typeof RequestIdSchema>;
1142 | export type JSONRPCRequest = Infer<typeof JSONRPCRequestSchema>;
1143 | export type JSONRPCNotification = Infer<typeof JSONRPCNotificationSchema>;
1144 | export type JSONRPCResponse = Infer<typeof JSONRPCResponseSchema>;
1145 | export type JSONRPCError = Infer<typeof JSONRPCErrorSchema>;
1146 | export type JSONRPCMessage = Infer<typeof JSONRPCMessageSchema>;
1147 | 
1148 | /* Empty result */
1149 | export type EmptyResult = Infer<typeof EmptyResultSchema>;
1150 | 
1151 | /* Cancellation */
1152 | export type CancelledNotification = Infer<typeof CancelledNotificationSchema>;
1153 | 
1154 | /* Initialization */
1155 | export type Implementation = Infer<typeof ImplementationSchema>;
1156 | export type ClientCapabilities = Infer<typeof ClientCapabilitiesSchema>;
1157 | export type InitializeRequest = Infer<typeof InitializeRequestSchema>;
1158 | export type ServerCapabilities = Infer<typeof ServerCapabilitiesSchema>;
1159 | export type InitializeResult = Infer<typeof InitializeResultSchema>;
1160 | export type InitializedNotification = Infer<typeof InitializedNotificationSchema>;
1161 | 
1162 | /* Ping */
1163 | export type PingRequest = Infer<typeof PingRequestSchema>;
1164 | 
1165 | /* Progress notifications */
1166 | export type Progress = Infer<typeof ProgressSchema>;
1167 | export type ProgressNotification = Infer<typeof ProgressNotificationSchema>;
1168 | 
1169 | /* Pagination */
1170 | export type PaginatedRequest = Infer<typeof PaginatedRequestSchema>;
1171 | export type PaginatedResult = Infer<typeof PaginatedResultSchema>;
1172 | 
1173 | /* Resources */
1174 | export type ResourceContents = Infer<typeof ResourceContentsSchema>;
1175 | export type TextResourceContents = Infer<typeof TextResourceContentsSchema>;
1176 | export type BlobResourceContents = Infer<typeof BlobResourceContentsSchema>;
1177 | export type Resource = Infer<typeof ResourceSchema>;
1178 | export type ResourceTemplate = Infer<typeof ResourceTemplateSchema>;
1179 | export type ListResourcesRequest = Infer<typeof ListResourcesRequestSchema>;
1180 | export type ListResourcesResult = Infer<typeof ListResourcesResultSchema>;
1181 | export type ListResourceTemplatesRequest = Infer<typeof ListResourceTemplatesRequestSchema>;
1182 | export type ListResourceTemplatesResult = Infer<typeof ListResourceTemplatesResultSchema>;
1183 | export type ReadResourceRequest = Infer<typeof ReadResourceRequestSchema>;
1184 | export type ReadResourceResult = Infer<typeof ReadResourceResultSchema>;
1185 | export type ResourceListChangedNotification = Infer<typeof ResourceListChangedNotificationSchema>;
1186 | export type SubscribeRequest = Infer<typeof SubscribeRequestSchema>;
1187 | export type UnsubscribeRequest = Infer<typeof UnsubscribeRequestSchema>;
1188 | export type ResourceUpdatedNotification = Infer<typeof ResourceUpdatedNotificationSchema>;
1189 | 
1190 | /* Prompts */
1191 | export type PromptArgument = Infer<typeof PromptArgumentSchema>;
1192 | export type Prompt = Infer<typeof PromptSchema>;
1193 | export type ListPromptsRequest = Infer<typeof ListPromptsRequestSchema>;
1194 | export type ListPromptsResult = Infer<typeof ListPromptsResultSchema>;
1195 | export type GetPromptRequest = Infer<typeof GetPromptRequestSchema>;
1196 | export type TextContent = Infer<typeof TextContentSchema>;
1197 | export type ImageContent = Infer<typeof ImageContentSchema>;
1198 | export type EmbeddedResource = Infer<typeof EmbeddedResourceSchema>;
1199 | export type PromptMessage = Infer<typeof PromptMessageSchema>;
1200 | export type GetPromptResult = Infer<typeof GetPromptResultSchema>;
1201 | export type PromptListChangedNotification = Infer<typeof PromptListChangedNotificationSchema>;
1202 | 
1203 | /* Tools */
1204 | export type Tool = Infer<typeof ToolSchema>;
1205 | export type ListToolsRequest = Infer<typeof ListToolsRequestSchema>;
1206 | export type ListToolsResult = Infer<typeof ListToolsResultSchema>;
1207 | export type CallToolResult = Infer<typeof CallToolResultSchema>;
1208 | export type CompatibilityCallToolResult = Infer<typeof CompatibilityCallToolResultSchema>;
1209 | export type CallToolRequest = Infer<typeof CallToolRequestSchema>;
1210 | export type ToolListChangedNotification = Infer<typeof ToolListChangedNotificationSchema>;
1211 | 
1212 | /* Logging */
1213 | export type LoggingLevel = Infer<typeof LoggingLevelSchema>;
1214 | export type SetLevelRequest = Infer<typeof SetLevelRequestSchema>;
1215 | export type LoggingMessageNotification = Infer<typeof LoggingMessageNotificationSchema>;
1216 | 
1217 | /* Sampling */
1218 | export type SamplingMessage = Infer<typeof SamplingMessageSchema>;
1219 | export type CreateMessageRequest = Infer<typeof CreateMessageRequestSchema>;
1220 | export type CreateMessageResult = Infer<typeof CreateMessageResultSchema>;
1221 | 
1222 | /* Autocomplete */
1223 | export type ResourceReference = Infer<typeof ResourceReferenceSchema>;
1224 | export type PromptReference = Infer<typeof PromptReferenceSchema>;
1225 | export type CompleteRequest = Infer<typeof CompleteRequestSchema>;
1226 | export type CompleteResult = Infer<typeof CompleteResultSchema>;
1227 | 
1228 | /* Roots */
1229 | export type Root = Infer<typeof RootSchema>;
1230 | export type ListRootsRequest = Infer<typeof ListRootsRequestSchema>;
1231 | export type ListRootsResult = Infer<typeof ListRootsResultSchema>;
1232 | export type RootsListChangedNotification = Infer<typeof RootsListChangedNotificationSchema>;
1233 | 
1234 | /* Client messages */
1235 | export type ClientRequest = Infer<typeof ClientRequestSchema>;
1236 | export type ClientNotification = Infer<typeof ClientNotificationSchema>;
1237 | export type ClientResult = Infer<typeof ClientResultSchema>;
1238 | 
1239 | /* Server messages */
1240 | export type ServerRequest = Infer<typeof ServerRequestSchema>;
1241 | export type ServerNotification = Infer<typeof ServerNotificationSchema>;
1242 | export type ServerResult = Infer<typeof ServerResultSchema>;
1243 |

10 |     "strict": true,
11 |     "esModuleInterop": true,
12 |     "forceConsistentCasingInFileNames": true,

14 |     "isolatedModules": true,

16 |   },
17 |   "include": ["src/**/*"],
18 |   "exclude": ["node_modules", "dist"]
19 | }
